# THE NUMBERS WALK — the fourth book in order from 1:1 (owner-ruled 2026-09-09 at THE TENT's close)

The owner's words (2026-09-09): "Let's go to the start of numbers and go through it in order" — asked first
"Did we not start at the first verse in numbers" (no: decision D11 had opened Numbers by the tent's four
cases, Lev 24, Num 9, 15, 27/36, the block closed as World/step9/THE_TENT.md). And the next sitting's
opening word: "OK, let's go starting with numbers the first first first verse." This file is the walk's map:
one section per sitting, the coverage computed at each close, the owed items named where they are found.

THE WALK'S FORM (the derivation era's, THE_STEPS Steps 2-5, at the PARASHAH GRAIN — speed ruling (a)):
each weekly portion is one READING SITTING (one pass over the spine — Onkelos whole, the Sifrei on Numbers
where it has a piska by POSITION — with the ledgers per block written by ONE script that computes its
coverage and its ink facts; one manifest script with every check cut from the store's bytes; one seat
script; the rituals in sequence; the corpus rebaked; the stamp row), then a COMPILE SITTING where the
portion's law and arithmetic are cold-compiled and put on the tape (World/step9/COMPILE_DEBT.md's THEN
NUMBERS box carries the owed list). The four case chapters (9, 15:32-41, 27, 36) are frozen and skipped
when the walk reaches them; their tape lines stand.

THE SHELF ON NUMBERS, MEASURED (sitting 1): the Sifrei on Numbers' export holds 161 piskaot; piska 1 opens
on 5:1; the LAST, 161, on 35:29 (no piska on 36 — THE TENT sitting 4); the only head inside chapters 1-4 is
piska 62's "(Bamidbar 3:24)", which by position (between 61 on 8:4 and 63 on 8:25) and by its own
quotations (8:24, 4:23) is the row on 8:24 — a mistyped digit, filed in RESEARCH_LOG.md; two heads carry no
parsable verse (59, 102) and are addressed by position too. Onkelos Numbers: 36 chapters, every verse.
The other 23 works on Numbers stay enumerated outside declared scope (each ledger names them).

## Sitting 1 — BAMIDBAR, Numbers 1:1-4:20 (2026-09-09): the reading and the units

THE DRAFTS: nine cover the portion exactly — computed: 159 of 159 verses, missing 0 (the state doc's note
had listed ten; the tenth, num_04_gershon_merari, opens at 4:21 — Naso's first verse, the parashah
boundary computed from the Tanakh DB's verse table). The nine: num_01_census_command 1:1-19,
num_01_tribe_counts 1:20-46, num_01_levites_exempt 1:47-54, num_02_camp_east_south 2:1-16,
num_02_camp_west_north 2:17-34, num_03_aaron_levi_replace 3:1-13, num_03_levite_clans_count 3:14-39,
num_03_firstborn_redeem 3:40-51, num_04_kehat 4:1-20.

THE READING (scratchpad write_bamidbar_ledgers.py → nine ledgers logic/oral_triage/<uid>_2026-09-09.md):
Onkelos Numbers 1:1-4:20 whole — 159 verses, MATERIAL 84 / CONTEXT 75 (the script's Counter) — plus the
Sifrei's piska 62 read fresh at num_04_kehat (MATERIAL: twenty-five to learn, thirty to serve; years
disqualify the Levites, blemishes do not — the a-fortiori from the priests REFUTED by "this is what
applies"): 160 sources, coverage computed, missing 0, extra 0, gloss_lint 0. THE HEBREW AND THE ARAMAIC
QUOTED ARE CUT BY CONSONANTS from the Tanakh DB and the shelf — a checker over every cut ran once and
found the two misses before the script did (a lesson: check every cut at once, not one per run).

THE INK, COMPUTED (every number from the ink's own numerals; every phrase's seats from the Tanakh DB):
- THE TWELVE COUNTS sum to 1:46's 603,550, which is 2:32's (the four camps 186,400 + 151,450 + 108,100 +
  157,600) and Exod 38:26's (the half-shekel count, computed on that verse) — one number, three seats, two
  books; each camp the sum of its three; the twelve numbers of chapter 1 repeated verbatim in chapter 2.
- THE LEVITE HOUSES 7,500 + 8,600 + 6,200 = 22,300 against 3:39's 22,000 — the delta of 300 written into
  the verses (the exam's answer is Bekhorot 5a's); the firstborn 22,273; the excess 273 (3:46); 273 × 5 =
  1,365 (3:50).
- THE CENSUS'S NUMBER GRAMMAR, measured on every number of the portion: a unit before hundreds multiplies;
  "thousand"/"thousands" WITHOUT the conjunction multiplies the group since the last thousands-word; "and a
  thousand" ADDS (three Torah seats: Exod 38:25, Num 3:50, 26:51); "from" and "a hundred of" are
  consonantal homographs told apart by their vowels (3:49-50 vs 2:9-31); a doubled numeral is distributive
  (3:47 "five, five"). THE ENGINE'S NUMERAL PARSER (SEQUENTIAL_RUN.md section 4) CANNOT READ IT — measured:
  cold_run_sequence.ink_numbers on 1:21 → 1,546 for 46,500; on 3:39 → 1,020 for 22,000; on 1:46 → two
  numbers for one (no thousands; "two" read as "years") — the compile sitting's first item.
- THE TRIBES' THREE ORDERS: the princes (1:5-15), the count (1:20-43, GAD moved from eleventh to THIRD),
  the camp (2:3-31, Judah's banner first); ONE PRINCE, TWO SPELLINGS — Deuel (1:14, 7:42, 7:47, 10:20)
  and Reuel (2:14, 10:29), Onkelos keeping each verse's own letter.
- THE ONE KETIV-QERE of the portion at 1:16 (the only unpointed token in 1:1-4:20 on the Tanakh DB and
  on the snapshot store — the store carries the written and the read side by side; Onkelos renders the
  read); the pedigree verb of 1:18 the Tanakh's one seat; the census idiom "lift the head" at 1:2 and
  26:2, "lift the head of" at 4:2 and 4:22.
- THE TEN UTTERANCE FRAMES (1:1, 1:48, 2:1, 3:5, 3:11, 3:14, 3:40, 3:44, 4:1, 4:17): three to Moses AND
  Aaron, two placed "in the wilderness of Sinai", one "said"; THE FIVE SPEC/RUN PAIRS (the census, the
  camp, the Levite count, the firstborn count, the redemption) with the formulas' seats; "by the mouth
  of the LORD" thrice in chapter 3.
- THE DATE STAMP 1:1 (the first of the second month, year two) beside Exod 40:17 (the first of the first
  month, year two) and Num 9:1 (the first month, year two): the tape's forward marker whose successor in
  the text is earlier in time — Pesachim 6b:7's own example; 1:18 repeats the date (the run on the
  command's day).
- THE STRANGER WHO APPROACHES SHALL BE PUT TO DEATH at four Torah seats (1:51, 3:10, 3:38, 18:7) —
  Onkelos: the LAY man, never the foreigner; the wrath-word's thirteen seats (1:53's purpose clause, 18:5
  the parallel); the charge-word's nine seats in the portion; the two rings (outer: east Judah, south
  Reuben, west Ephraim, north Dan; inner: west Gershon, south Kohath, north Merari, east Moses-Aaron)
  and the march's four ordinals in the ink's own words.
- THE GENESIS HEADING "these are the generations" — nine Genesis seats and Num 3:1 (Aaron and Moses),
  the list naming Aaron's sons alone; "and sons they had not" (3:4) the Tanakh's one seat; "whose hand
  he filled" (3:3) rendered by Onkelos as "whose offering was brought near" — Lev 8's rite named; "opener
  of the womb" (3:12) the firstborn engine's own token (five Torah seats); "given, given" (3:9) rendered
  by two verbs; "twenty gerah the shekel" (3:47, four Torah seats) rendered "twenty ma'in the sela" — the
  conversion layer's words (Bekhorot 50a) at their Numbers seat; "the prince of the princes" (3:32)
  rendered by the Temple's office-title, the amarkal; "wholly of blue" (4:6) the high priest's robe's
  phrase on the ark alone; "as it is swallowed" (4:20) the Tanakh's one seat, rendered "when they cover
  the vessels".

THE CLAIMS (scratchpad write_bamidbar_manifests.py → nine manifests, 32 claims, 32 machine checks, every

prediction matched; VERIFY GREEN. THE STAMP: one delegated FULL RULE row for the nine (logic/findings/
STAMP_LEDGER.md, 2026-09-09). No engine file changed at this sitting — the sweep and the journal gate stand
as at THE TENT's close (42/42 at 4,633; the journal gate GREEN).

OWED FROM SITTING 1 (the compile sitting; also in COMPILE_DEBT's THEN NUMBERS box): (a) the engine's

## Sitting 1b — THE COMPILE OF BAMIDBAR (the design, 2026-09-09; the owner: "Go"; written before the types, the
## declaration, the probes' pass, the code — after the reading, the freeze and the exam docket)

THE MEASUREMENTS FIRST (the tent's lesson, every claim of "already there" grepped):
- THE PARSER: cold_run_sequence.ink_numbers has no thousands word beyond a bare אלף ("thousand") that ADDS 1,000 and
  no אלפים ("thousands"); it reads שנים ("two") as "years" unless before עשר ("ten"); it reads מאת ("from", the
  preposition) as a hundred (a latent Genesis bug: Gen 23:20 "from the sons of Heth" parses as 100 — no marker sat on
  such a verse, so it never fired). census_probes.py, written first, runs 3/17 on the unchanged engine (the census
  probes N1-N11 FAIL; R3 FAILS on the old homograph; R1, R2, R4 pass and must stay passing).
- THE CELLS TO CALL: cold_run_pesach.firstborn({'kind': 'human'}, data) -> 'redeem (five sela — fetched constant)'
  with effects consecrated_firstborn + pays (the five the data channel, Num 18:16's); cold_run_incense_shekel.shekel
  ('twenty_gerah') -> the five Torah seats of "twenty gerah" with 3:47 among them (the unit defined in the verse);
  cold_run_sanctuary_build.books('conversion_layer') -> Onkelos' selaim on the erection's accounts (38:24-26). No
  runner computes 603,550 (grep: none) — the erection's shekel count is parsed here on Exod 38:26 and compared.
- THE RECORDER AND THE STITCHER: scratchpad/seq_record.py imports every cold_run_*.py and captures the scenes'
  submits (42 modules today; 43 with this runner); scratchpad/seq_stitch.py filters to HISTORY by the register test,
  dedups, re-bases, MERGES THE MARKERS (its table verified against the parser at stitch time — the parser change is
  checked here on every marker verse of the tape before it reaches the runner) and writes the tape between the
  runner's sentinels; SPAN_ORDER gets 'bamidbar'.
- THE TAPE'S NUM 9 MARKER: mk('Num 9:5', 'F', [14], ... day_in('exodus', 2, 1, 14)) — text-constrained, class F
  (FORWARD from the erection when the tape knew no earlier Numbers date). With Num 1:1's marker at (2, 2, 1) ahead of
  it in tape order, the engine's marker() finds day < counter and logs RETROGRADE (the counter unmoved, `dated` on the
  following events until the clock next moves — the reprieve's path, already built). The row's class is retyped 'R';
  the census F/P/R moves by the stitcher's print. THE MEN'S DUE: set at 9:6-7's submission with the counter at
  (2, 2, 1) for (2, 2, 14) — a future due, set normally; fired on the daughters' marker's walk as before (CN1 holds).
  THE ERECTION'S MORROW TIMERS now fire on the walk to (2, 2, 1) (the 1:1 marker) instead of on the walk to (2, 1, 14):
  the same fires, an earlier line; the fired count unmoved.
- THE DOTS OVER AARON (Bekhorot 4a:11): the XML carries the puncta extraordinaria on וְׄאַׄהֲׄרֹ֛ׄןׄ ("and Aaron", 3:39)
  as U+05C4 marks, and THE SNAPSHOT STORE CARRIES THEM (the token at idx 6 — unlike the large letters, which it drops):
  a data row the compiled function reads off the store's own bytes.
- THE DOCKET (logic/oral_triage/num_01_04_bamidbar_exam_2026-09-09.md): 159 rows — LAW 46, DERIVATION 31, DISPUTE 13,
  CONTEXT 16, OUTSIDE 53; the link-driven 55 (24 LAW) and the topic windows 104. Two pointers corrected there: the two
  thousand cubits are NOT derived from 2:2 (Eruvin 51a runs Exod 16:29 -> Num 35:5); the Jerusalem Talmud's
  box-or-beam on 2:17 was not found in its window (a hypothesis of the seat, OPEN).

THE PARSER'S RULE (SEQUENTIAL_RUN.md section 4 amended; ONE copy of the parser — the stitcher execs the INK block):
a numeral word IDENTICAL to the numeral word before it is DISTRIBUTIVE (3:47 "five, five" = 5; Gen 7:2 "seven,
seven" = 7 — a behavior change off every marker verse); a unit immediately before מאת/מאות ("hundred") multiplies
(unchanged); אלף/אלפים ("thousand"/"thousands") WITHOUT the conjunction multiplies the GROUP since the last
thousands-word (an empty group: אלף = 1,000, אלפים = 2,000 — the dual, Num 35:5's "two thousand cubits"); with the
conjunction, וְאֶלֶף ("and a thousand") ADDS (Exod 38:25, Num 3:50, 26:51); שנים ("two") is a numeral before
עשר/עשרה AND before וְעֶשְׂרִים ("and twenty" — 3:39, 3:43, 26:14, 26:62), else "years" (unchanged); THE HOMOGRAPH:
verse_words keeps the points' one witness — the word pointed מֵאֵת (tsere: "from") is emitted as מ-את, never a
numeral, and מְאַת ("a hundred of") as מאת (the stitcher and the runner share the function). The year-word and the
noun-boundary rules are unchanged. THE GATE: census_probes.py 17/17; the stitcher's marker verification (every marker
verse's parse equals its typed numbers — a changed parse on a marker verse is an INK MISMATCH that stops the stitch
and is READ, then the marker's numbers retyped from the ink); the runner's assert_ink at run time on every marker.

THE FUNCTION (cold_run_bamidbar.py — five cells, the five motions, the honest-pairing guard, effects on every
verdict, every token probed):
- F1 census(case, data): the twelve counts and the total (the ink's numbers by the engine's parser, live); the sum
  against 1:46, 2:32 and Exod 38:26 (the three seats); the threshold twenty and "everyone going out to the host"; the
  count of NAMES per head; lineage follows the father (1:2 — Bava Batra 109b, Bekhorot 47a, Nazir 49a; Kiddushin
  69a's mamzeret exception recorded); the princes designated in the blasphemer's lemma (Sanhedrin 56a); the tribes'
  three orders; the Levites exempt from the count; the date stamp and the retrograde (Pesachim 6b:7; Rav Pappa's
  bound); the New Moon of 9:1 by the verbal analogy (Pesachim 6b:6 — a reading-placed date, its teacher named: the
  data row num9_command_day).
- F2 levites(case, data): the substitution (3:12 "opener of the womb" — the firstborn engine's clause, CALLED for the
  human firstborn's verdict; 3:13 dated to the plague's night); the houses' counts and the total AS WRITTEN — the delta
  300 computed and answered (Bekhorot 5a:8-9: firstborn Levites redeem no one; Aaron not counted — the dots); the
  firstborn 22,273; the excess 273; five shekels per skull — the shekel CALLED (the twenty gerah; Onkelos' sela and
  ma'ah; Mishnah Bekhorot 8:7's Tyrian maneh); 273 x 5 = 1,365 to Aaron and his sons; the lots (Sanhedrin 17a); the
  redemption's rows — the two-headed firstborn ten sela (Menachot 37b), the means and the moment (Mishnah Bekhorot
  8:8), after thirty days (Bekhorot 49a), the gentile partner exempt (3:13, Mishnah Bekhorot 1:1, 2:1), the priests
  and Levites exempt from the son and the donkey but not the kosher animal (3:45's a-fortiori; 3:12's "shall be" for
  the generations — Bekhorot 4b:1), one Levite lamb for many donkeys (Bekhorot 4b:6-9); THE PARAMETER ROW
  wilderness_firstborn_sanctity (R. Yochanan: sanctified and remained; Reish Lakish: until the census — Bekhorot
  4b:11-5a:7, the reversed attribution recorded); the generations of Aaron and Moses (Sanhedrin 19b:17); the
  sonlessness (Yevamot 64a).
- F3 camp(case, data): the four banners by the ink's compass words and the march's four ordinals; the tent in the
  midst; "as they camp so they journey"; the tent on the march still the tent (Zevachim 61b:3, 116b:16-17 — the meat
  not disqualified); the three camps mapped onto Jerusalem (Zevachim 116b:14-15); "beside him" = beside (Menachot
  96a:11 — M-22's shape, the teacher Abba Shaul; the TEIKU at 27a:2 carried); THE PARAMETER ROW showbread_on_the_march
  (disqualified by 2:17's juxtaposition / not, by 4:7's "continual" — Menachot 95a:4-5); the distance a DATUM (2:2;
  the two thousand cubits' seat Num 35:5 — the docket's corrected pointer).
- F4 charges(case, data): the three houses' charges (soft / holy / hard) and the inner ring; the charge-word; the
  wrath-shield; the stranger's three readings (1:51 the non-Levite, 3:10 the non-priest — THE PARAMETER ROW
  zar_who_served: death by Heaven (the Rabbis, the running setting) / strangulation (R. Akiva) — Mishnah Sanhedrin
  9:6; 3:38 the Levite in another Levite's service — Arakhin 11b); the priests' three watches (Tamid 26a); the
  altars plural — the golden like the ground (Chagigah 27a); THE AGES TABLE (Chullin 24a:7-12; the Sifrei's piska
  62): priests unfit by blemish, Levites by years; the years only while carrying; twenty-five to learn, thirty to
  serve, fifty to return; the thresholds twenty / a month (and a day — Arakhin 18b) / thirty-to-fifty; the amarkal
  (Mishnah Shekalim 5:2 — seven; Onkelos' word on Eleazar).
- F5 kohath(case, data): the packing's order (the priests cover, THEN the carriers); the layers (blue outside only the
  ark; the bronze altar ashed and purple; the hide outermost); the bread on the table on the march (4:7; Menachot
  99b:11 the first teacher / R. Yosei — THE ROW bread_always); the vessels sanctified by service for the generations
  (Sanhedrin 16b, Shevuot 15a); a vessel in a vessel proper (Yoma 58a); the kasva thief killed by zealots (Sanhedrin
  81b — 4:7's jugs, 4:20's "swallowed"); not to see the packing, even the Levites (Yoma 54a); the three death clauses;
  cut not off; the tachash (Shabbat 28b:6 — THE DATA ROW tachash_identity beside Onkelos' sasgona).
THE DATA ROWS (motion 2, the settings recorded): wilderness_firstborn_sanctity; zar_who_served; showbread_on_the_march;
bread_always; threshold_edge (a month and a day); aaron_not_counted (the puncta); the_lots; tachash_identity;
num9_command_day (Pesachim 6b:6, the New Moon); altar_height (ten cubits by 3:26's juxtaposition, Shabbat 92a —
the sanctuary engine's parameter, a pointer recorded here, not set).

THE DAEMON law_census (the 48th; file cold_run_bamidbar.py; wraps bamidbar; given_at Num 1:1; installed_by boot —
the standing setting for a law spoken at its verse with no installing act on the tape; D2's second pass may move it
to the utterance). THE KINDS (13 on the tape, in the registry's two forms): SPEECH — census_commanded (1:1-19's
frame: the threshold, by names, the twelve aides; the marker's verse), levites_exempted (1:48-53: the exemption, the
appointment, the stranger clause, the wrath-shield), camp_commanded (2:1-2), levites_given (3:5-13: brought near,
given, instead of the firstborn — the substitution declared, the ground dated), levite_count_commanded (3:14-15),
firstborn_count_commanded (3:40-41), redemption_commanded (3:44-48), kohath_service_commanded (4:1-20); ACTS —
census_taken (1:17-19: the counts as data fields, the pedigrees, the date repeated), camp_arrayed (2:34: camped and
journeyed), levites_counted (3:16, 3:39: the houses and the total as written), firstborn_counted (3:42-43),
firstborn_redeemed (3:49-51: the excess, the rate, the money, the recipients). THE CASE KINDS for the exam's scene:
stranger_approached (who: non_levite / non_priest / levite_in_another_service), levite_service_case (who, blemish,
age, carrying), firstborn_redemption_case (the docket's rows: heads, means, partner, owner, animal, age_days). THE
EFFECTS (the tradition's vocabulary; new: commanded — a debit the run closes; counted — a status with the number;
arrayed_by_banners; given_to_aaron; substituted_for_the_firstborn; redeemed; reused: exempt, charge_kept,
appointed_to_serve, pays, consecrated_firstborn, put_to_death): each SPEECH writes `commanded` on its addressee (the
spec as an open debit); each ACT writes the status and CLOSES the debit — THE FIVE SPEC/RUN PAIRS AS FIVE
OPEN-THEN-CLOSED DEBITS, the ink's own structure on the ledger. THE ENTITIES: the_levites (kind people; the tribe,
not Levi the person), the_firstborn_of_israel (kind people; the 22,273 as a class) — registered before the code;
israel, aaron-and-sons, the-tabernacle stand. THE EDGES: bamidbar -> pesach CALL reference (3:12's "opener of the
womb" = Exod 13:2, 12; the human firstborn's verdict), bamidbar -> incense_shekel CALL reference (3:47's "twenty
gerah" = Exod 30:13), bamidbar -> sanctuary_build CALL reference (the conversion layer; 38:26's count), sequence ->
bamidbar CALL none.

THE TAPE (the stitcher): the 1:1 marker mk('Num 1:1', 'F', [1], ords=[2], "M['bamidbar'] = day_in('exodus', 2, 2, 1)")
text-constrained (the parse of 1:1 typed from the stitcher's own print — the ordinal table reads השני and not the

took, 3:51 gave; every speech within ten verses of one). THE CHECKPOINTS (declared here, computed by the engine from
the tape, a miss reported never repaired): CB1 the twelve summed = 1:46's parse; CB2 = Exod 38:26's parse (the
erection's seat); CB3 the houses' sum − 3:39's = 300 (Bekhorot 5a:8, the question; 5a:9 the answer as the cell's
verdict); CB4 3:43 − 3:39 = 3:46 (273); CB5 273 × 5 = 3:50 (1,365); CB6 the 1:1 marker's day = exodus (2, 2, 1) and
the counter after it; CB7 the Num 9:5 marker logged RETROGRADE (Pesachim 6b:7 — the counter unmoved, the men's due
still (2, 2, 14): CN1 MATCH stands); CB8 the five debits opened by the speeches and closed by the acts (five closes).
THE PREDICTION (typed from the stitcher's print and the code's own arithmetic BEFORE the run): events +13, markers
+1 (133; F unchanged 115 after 9:5's retype and 1:1's add; R 2 -> 3), timers set unchanged, fired unchanged, writes

by script -> the declaration (the daemon block, the edges) -> the daemon gate to FAIL (the function undeclared) ->

## Sitting 1b — as built (2026-09-09)

THE DOCKET FIRST (logic/oral_triage/num_01_04_bamidbar_exam_2026-09-09.md): 159 rows read and verdicted by one script from
the shelf scan — LAW 46, DERIVATION 31, DISPUTE 13, CONTEXT 16, OUTSIDE 53; gloss_lint 0. THE PROBES TO FAIL: census_probes.py
3/17 on the unchanged parser (the census rows all FAIL; R3 exposed the old parser's own homograph — Gen 23:20 "from the sons
of Heth" read as a hundred). THE TYPES by script (scratchpad add_types_bamidbar.py): 16 kinds (registry 830), 6 effects (921),
2 entities (281), the daemon block law_census (48), the functions block bamidbar (5 cells WRAPPED), the span and four edges,
installation_probes I5 47 -> 48. THE GATES TO FAIL: the daemon gate 2 (the def missing; the function undeclared), the
dependency gate 4 (the CALL edges without live imports) — as designed.

THE PARSER, in five passes each READ from an instrument: (1) the census's grammar (thousands multiply the group; the
addend; the distributive; the plural אלפים) — 16/17, the article on each part of 3:46 missing; (2) the article admitted —
17/17, and THE STITCHER'S MARKER VERIFICATION FIRED on two Genesis markers ("Kiriath-arba" and "the years of plenty" read as
numbers) — the article narrowed to a chain; (3) the runner's own assert on the twelve summed found 4,000 missing — "two and
thirty", "two and sixty" (the rule knew only "two and twenty") — generalized; (4) THE CORPUS-WIDE DIFF, old parser against new
on every verse of the four books (131 moved), read verse by verse: the homograph regex had caught מֵאֹת ("hundreds", the
plural's holam — Genesis 5's "after" verses, and checkpoint C0 DIVERGED); the distributive had merged "a hundred AND a
hundred" (Lev 26:8) and "a thousand AND a thousand" (Num 26:51); the article form had counted "and the OTHER" (Gen 42:13,
Lev 14:22); the dual "thousands" had counted "rulers of THOUSANDS" (Exod 18:21); and the positional "two" rule had read
"second and third decks" (Gen 6:16) as thirty-two; (5) THE POINTS ON THE STEM, measured on the DB's own code points: מֵאֵת
("from") has the tsere under BOTH letters; the numeral "two" always has a sheva under the shin (שְׁנַיִם, שְׁנֵים, the pausal
שְׁנָיִם), "years" a qamats (שָׁנִים), "second ones" a hiriq under the nun — verse_words now emits the non-numeral pointing
with a star and the parser keeps the phrase open on it; the doubling on the RAW word (no conjunction); "and the N" only
inside a chain; the dual only before a numeral. The final diff (155 verses moved, none a marker): the census verses of
Numbers 1, 2, 26, 31 (61); the thousands elsewhere (Exod 12:37 and Num 11:21 "six hundred thousand on foot" — 1,600 before;
Num 17:14, 25:9, the Levites' second count 26:62; Naso's 4:36-48; the princes' offerings 7:85); the "from" homograph
silenced at forty seats; and the bare numeral "two" the old parser never saw (Exod 22:3 "he shall pay two", 25:18 "two
cherubim", Num 28:3 "two lambs daily", Gen 7:2 "the unclean by two") — the old positional rule had called every one of
them "years". census_probes.py 17/17; the stitcher's 133 markers verified; the tape section byte-identical before the
new lines joined.

it, as the full run would, before the world stops (a retrograde or proleptic one closes nothing, as in the run) — cursor
6/6. THE LIMIT MEASURED AND RECORDED (THE_LOOP.md "The cursor's bound rule"): a cursor INSIDE a bound whose closing marker
lies beyond it (Num 15:32) is REFUSED, because the bound's right edge is in the chained bytes; the cursor stands at a forward
marker's verse or after the last marker's bound has closed. THE JOURNAL GATE GREEN — the four views MATCH on all four worlds
(the running world: ledger 1277 = writes, timers 44 = sets, clock 133 = markers, docket 4 open 0; THE REST 1257 / 132). THE
SWEEP: 43/43 runners green, 4,699 graded cells (4,633 + this runner's 66). The corpus hash 8b8fff1fa28953af unmoved (no
unit touched at this sitting).

⚠ LESSONS (1b): A PARSER IS TAUGHT AGAINST THE WHOLE CORPUS — the probes prove the census, the stitcher's verification
proves the markers, and only the old-against-new diff over every verse shows what else moved (four rules read off it).
THE POINTS ON THE STEM DECIDE A HOMOGRAPH — measure the code points before writing the regex (the shin-dot precedes the
vowel; a dagesh ("the doubling dot") may sit between). AN ENTITY ALREADY ON THE LEDGER UNDER A RAW TOKEN IS RE-HOMED BY A REGISTRY ROW (the
Levites since the calf) — grep the tape for the token before predicting the entity count. THE CURSOR AT A MARKER CLOSES
THE BOUNDS BEHIND IT; a cursor inside a bound is refused by construction. THE DOCKET'S TOPIC WINDOWS ARE ADDRESSED, NOT
SEARCHED — a regex over a tractate's English returned 106 rows of noise. THE DEPENDENCY GATE'S NEW EDGES ARE READ ONE BY
ONE — two homographs (תַּחַת "instead of" / the talion's "for"; Shelumiel / the peace offerings), one owed reference (the
continual meal-offering), six run-citations. THE HAND'S NUMBERS: 62 for the guard's 66; three debits for the code's four;
the year in the date tuple; 262 entities for 261 — each retyped from its instrument.

THE INK, COMPUTED (every claim of the hand an assert against the Tanakh DB and the snapshot store — NINE FAILED on the
module's first run and were read one by one: two sort orders; Poti-phera's second half caught by the uncover-root
search; the final letters in the belly/thigh test; the MARK ORDER of a typed pointed form against the DB's bytes (a
consonant-groups parse replaced the string compare); the fifth-clause's plural at Lev 5:24; the article on
"jealousies"; Pedahzur's two tokens at 10:23 where the hand had said one — assert_driver.py runs a module statement by
statement and lists every failing assert at once):
- THE FOUR WORK-COUNTS BY THE ENGINE'S OWN PARSER (taught at sitting 1b — its first proof on new ink): Kohath 2,750,
  Gershon 2,630, Merari 3,200 — summed 8,580 = 4:48; the dual אלפים ("thousands") as two thousand, Onkelos writing the
  two (תרין, "two"); against chapter 3's houses the shares are 32, 35, 52 of a hundred — the smallest house the
  largest share, the frame-carriers.
- FOUR PARSER GAPS MEASURED ON THE PORTION'S OWN NUMBERS: the construct "two of" (שני / שתי) not a numeral (7:3
  [6, 10, 1] for [6, 12, 2, 1]; 7:7 [4] for [2, 4]; 6:10 [] for [2, 2]); עשתי עשר ("eleven") unknown (7:72 — seven
  Torah seats); "one" fused to the number after it (7:13 [131, 1, 70] for [1, 130, 1, 70]); and 7:14's "one, ten"
  read as ELEVEN. THE ACCENTS DECIDE IT: the "one" carries a TEVIR, a disjunctive, at all twelve pan-verses
  (7:14 ... 7:80), while every true "eleven" written one-and-ten in the Tanakh (Gen 32:23, Deut 1:2, Josh 15:51,
  2 Kgs 9:29) joins its "one" to its "ten" by a conjunctive or the maqqef; 7:13's "one" carries a revia; AND THE
  TOTAL PROVES THE ACCENTS — 7:86's "all the gold of the pans, a hundred and twenty" = 12 × 10 (12 × 11 = 132), the
  very total the Sifrei uses to decide gold-versus-silver (piskaot 49, 55): M-26 THE ACCENT READ (MOVE_CATALOG). The
  totals of 7:84-88 all read right: [12, 12, 12] (מֵאֵת "from" no number — 1b's homograph at its Naso seat);
  [130, 70, 2400] = 12 × 130 + 12 × 70 (the dual before "and four"); [12, 10, 120] ("ten, ten" the distributive);
  12 × (1, 1, 1, 1); [24, 60, 60, 60] = 12 × 7:17's [2, 5, 5, 5].
- THE TWELVE OFFERINGS ARE ONE TEXT (diffed word for word): the differences are the opening word ("and his offering"
  7:13; "he brought near his offering" 7:19 — Nethanel alone; "his offering" 7:25 on), the prince's name, and one
  spelling (עתודים "he-goats" plene at 7:17, 7:23; defective at the other ten); the day-heads' three forms (Nahshon
  "of the tribe of Judah" untitled; Nethanel with a verb and "prince of Issachar"; the ten "prince of the sons of X";
  the eleventh and twelfth with "day" appended); THE TWELVE DAYS IN THE CAMP'S ORDER (= 2:3-31, computed); Deuel with
  the dalet at 7:42, 7:47; Pedahzur two tokens at 7:54, 7:59, 10:23 on both stores, one at 1:10, 2:20.
- THE BLESSING: 3, 5, 7 words; 15, 20, 25 letters; the Name thrice (computed); "thus shall you bless", "say to them",
  "make His face shine", "lift His face" each one seat.
- THREE HOMOGRAPHS BY THE POINTS ON THE STEM (1b's law at three new words, measured on the DB's marks): מַעַל
  ("trespass", 13 seats) / מֵעַל ("from upon", 191); מִשְׁרַת ("steeping", 6:3, one) / מְשָׁרֵת ("minister", 7);
  מִדַּבֵּר ("speaking itself" — the reflexive at 7:89, 8 seats) / מְדַבֵּר ("speaking", 26) / מִדְבָּר ("wilderness",
  45) — one consonantal word, three words; Onkelos renders 7:89's participle AND its closing active verb reflexively.
- THE DATE OF 7:1 ("on the day Moses finished setting up" — one seat; Exod 40:33 the erection's finishing) = Exod
  40:17 = Lev 9:1 = exodus (2, 1, 1), a MONTH BEFORE 1:1's (2, 2, 1): the tape's second retrograde marker inside
  Numbers; the twelve days 7:12-83 = 1-12 Nisan; the second Passover's men on the 14th (Num 9:6) — one month's tape,
  three markers. The frames: 8 (4:21, 5:1, 5:5, 5:11, 6:1, 6:22, 7:4, 7:11 — two "said"); 6:23 addresses Aaron and
  his sons inside Moses' frame; the register 23 verses (7:1 five narrative verbs).
- THE SUSPECTED WIFE'S OWN WORDS: "no witness against her", "not seized", "a reminder of iniquity", "an offering of
  memorial", "holy water", "a curse and an oath", "sown with seed", "bear her iniquity" each one seat; the ephah spelled
  plene here as at Exod 16:36, Num 28:5 against Leviticus' defective; קמח ("meal") at two Torah seats (Sarah's and
  this); the uncover-root's six Torah seats; thigh-then-belly at 5:21, belly-then-thigh at 5:22 and 5:27; the bare
  "amen, amen" at Num 5:22 and Neh 8:6 alone; "and erase" at two Torah seats (Deut 29:19's blotted NAME); the
  defilement-root's eight tokens (the one with the conjunction at 5:29); "this is the law" at 5:29, 6:13, 6:21 among
  the Torah's fourteen; the camp-word thrice in 5:2-3 (the Sifrei's three camps counted on the ink).
- THE NAZIRITE'S OWN WORDS: the declaring verb's four Torah seats; נזיר ("nazirite") at nine (Joseph's two, the
  vine's one, the chapter's six); נזר ("crown") at five — the high priest's four and the nazirite's one; "kernels" and
  "skin", "a razor shall not pass", "in a sudden instant", "the first days shall fall", "he shall bring him[self]",
  "after, the nazirite may drink wine", "under the peace sacrifice" each one seat; "upon a dead soul he shall not come"
  with the high priest's pair at Lev 21:11; "holy" at 6:5, 6:8, 6:20.
- THE TRANSLATION'S INSERTIONS, cut from the shelf's bytes: THE TITHE (5:10 "the tithe of his holy things"); THREE
  SE'AH for the ephah (5:15 — the conversion layer on a DRY measure after the shekel-to-sela of 3:47 and 7:13); THE
  LAVER for "holy water" (5:17 — the Sifrei's word too); THE POT under the fire (6:18 — the Sifrei's kitchen); "THE
  BLESSING OF My name" (6:27 — against the Sifrei's Name itself); "when COVERED" (7:3 — Rebbi's arm); "My SHEKHINAH"
  for "I dwell" (5:3 — the Sifrei's noun); ONE WORD, TWO RENDERINGS a verse apart (6:25 "His Shekhinah" / 6:26 "His
  face"); me'ilah rendered LYING at 5:6, 5:12, 5:27 — the Sifrei's lemma rule ("me'ilah in all places is lying") is
  the Aramaic word; "new and old" for wine and strong drink (6:3 — a third reading beside the Sifrei's two, recorded);
  "upon the dead" for "the soul" (6:11 — R. Yishmael's arm); "he shall bring himself" (6:13 — R. Yishmael's
  reflexive); "he shall not enter" (6:6 — the tent-uncleanness reading); waving rendered LIFTING (5:25, 6:20).

THE SIFREI'S OWN CASE LAW (eight entries appended to MIDDOT.md "The middot's own case law"): the rule of repetition

— "if both can be satisfied the a-fortiori stands", and its output MERIT SUSPENDS the waters, a four-setting parameter
(8:1); a general that adds to the particular (24:1); the three-facet paradigm (6:1); the circular a-fortiori failing
and the identity on an EXTRA word deciding (25:1, 31:3 — 6:20's "nazirite", the clause's one seat); I11 (37:1) and
I13 (58:1) stated on their verses; the crossed sotah/nazirite parameter (28:1). Other verdict tables and parameters
for the compile: the secreting's minimum (six teachers, 7:3); the ladder of stringency and the three camps (1:4, 1:7);
the blessing's form (39:1); the sealed decree's eight pairs (42:2); the prince's four exceptions (51:1); the
leper-nazirite's four refusals (38:1); tumah voids all, shaving thirty (31:1); which days count (31:2); the thirty
days three ways (22:1, 25:1); David's wagon — the rule run in Samuel and Chronicles (46:2).

THE MEASUREMENTS FIRST (every claim of "already there" grepped; every number by a script):
- THE PARSER, re-measured on the unchanged engine: the reading's gaps stand — 7:3 [6, 10, 1] for [6, 12, 2, 1]; 7:7 [4] for
  [2, 4]; 6:10 [] for [2, 2] AND A FALSE ORDINAL — ink_ordinals reads 6:10's "two young pigeons" as the ordinal 2 ([8, 2] for
  [8]); 7:13 [131, 1, 70] for [1, 130, 1, 70]; 7:14 [11] for [1, 10]; 7:72 [10] for [11]; the day-heads 7:12-66 read RIGHT by
  their ordinals ([1] ... [10]) and 7:78 by its numeral ([12]); the totals 7:84-88 right. THE ACCENT CENSUS over the four books
  (every numeral word followed by a numeral word WITHOUT the conjunction): 325 pairs — 202 with a conjunctive on the first, 34
  with none (the maqqef), 89 with a DISJUNCTIVE; of the 89, sixty-one are "N THOUSAND" (a tevir, tipcha or pashta on the tens
  before אלף "thousand" — the thousand multiplies its group: a cut there breaks the census), three are article forms the parser
  already sets aside (Exod 26:2, 26:8, 36:9, 36:15 "the one [curtain]"), and TWENTY-FIVE are "ONE": the twelve dish-verses
  (7:13 ... 7:79, a revia on "one" before "thirty and a hundred"), the twelve pan-verses (7:14 ... 7:80, the tevir before "ten"),
  and Num 28:27 "one ram, seven lambs" (an etnachta — read today as [2, 8]: the fused "one" at a THIRD seat, a true correction
  the reading did not know). SO M-26'S RULE IS KEYED ON "ONE": אחד / אחת ("one", with or without the vav) carrying a disjunctive
  accent and followed by a numeral word without the conjunction ENDS its number (verse_words emits it with a bar; ink_numbers
  closes the phrase after it); with a conjunctive, a maqqef or no accent it joins — every true eleven so joined (Gen 32:23 a
  merkha, Deut 1:2 a qadma, 2 Kgs 9:29 a munach (the "resting" conjunctive), Josh 15:51 the maqqef). THE CONSTRUCT BY THE POINTS ON THE STEM (1b's law at
  a fourth word, measured on the DB's marks): שְׁנֵי / שְׁתֵּי ("two of" — a sheva under the shin AND a tsere under the second
  letter; the morphology's HAcmdc / HAcfdc, 228 and 139 seats in the Tanakh with their ו/ב/ל/מ prefixes) against שֵׁנִי
  ("second" — a tsere under the shin), שָׁנִי ("scarlet"), שְׁנִי ("scarlet of", Lev 14:4 — a sheva under the shin but a HIRIQ
  under the nun), שִׁנֵּי ("the teeth of"), שְׁתִי ("the warp", Lev 13), שְׁתִּי (Vashti) and the verbs — every one told by the
  second letter's point; emitted with a caret (שני^, שתי^) so the ordinal reader keeps "second" and the numeral reader takes
  "two of". ELEVEN: עַשְׁתֵּי ("eleven", the bound form before עשר / עשרה — Exod 26:7-8, 36:14-15, Num 7:72, 29:20, Deut 1:3 and
  thirteen seats of the Prophets and Writings) is the unit 1 that joins its ten; the verb spelled the same (2 Sam 7:9, 23:17 —
  a sin-dot) told by the point. THE GATE: census_probes rows written to FAIL first (7:3, 7:7, 6:10 with its ordinal, 7:13,

- THE TAPE'S MARKERS AND THE REGISTER'S VERDICT (measured by the stitcher's own test): 7:1's "the day Moses finished setting up
  the tabernacle" carries no numeral — its day is the erection's (M['erected'], Exod 40:17 = Lev 9:1 = exodus (2, 1, 1); the
  day-stack of Sifrei 44:1 and Shabbat 87b's ten crowns), stated AFTER 1:1's (2, 2, 1): RETROGRADE. THE TWELVE DAY-HEADS are
  the text's own stamps — 7:12 ... 7:66 by the ordinals 1 ... 10, 7:72 by [11] (after the fix), 7:78 by [12] — twelve more
  retrograde markers at erected + (n − 1): thirteen new R rows (3 -> 16), F 115 unmoved. THE REGISTER: 4:34 ויפקד ("and he
  counted"), 5:4 ויעשו ("and they did"), 7:1 ויהי ("and it was"), 7:2 ויקריבו ("and they brought near"), 7:3 ויביאו ("and
  they brought"), 7:6 ויקח ("and he took"), 7:10 ויקריבו, 7:12 ויהי, 7:89 וישמע ("and he heard") are narrative verbs; 7:4 and
  7:11 ויאמר ("and He said") are speech frames; THE DAY-BLOCKS 7:18-83 CARRY NO NARRATIVE VERB OF THEIR OWN (7:18's הקריב "he
  brought near" a perfect; the third day and after outside any ten-verse window) — by the engine's own convention the twelve
  offerings are NOT tape lines. THEY ARE THE SCHEDULE: the command at 7:11 ("one prince per day, one prince per day") sets
  twelve dues from the anointing day, inside the retrograde stretch, and the engine writes a past due AT SUBMISSION as a
  RETRO-WRITE (the seventh log class, first exercised on a schedule): twelve retro-writes, one on each prince's entity with
  his day and his offering's numbers; the checkpoint compares each retro-write's due with its day-head marker's stated day —
  the schedule's arithmetic and the ink's ordinals computed apart. The four law sections (5:5-10, 5:11-31, 6:1-21, 6:22-27) are
  NOT tape lines (as Leviticus' laws are not): their frames are speeches without runs; they live as cells and case kinds.
- THE REGISTRY: the twelve princes have no row (grep: none; the frozen units carry one expression token, num_07's
  on_the_first_day_nahshon) — THIRTEEN entities registered before the code: the_princes_of_israel (kind people — the twelve as
  a body, 7:2's "the princes of Israel, the heads of their fathers' houses, they who stood over the counted") and the twelve
  persons in the camp's order (nahshon ... ahira), each with a step9-scenes member; the frozen units' tokens a later join. A
  fourteenth: the_unclean_of_the_camp (kind people — 5:2's three classes as one body, sent out at 5:4).
- THE DOCKET SIZED (scratchpad naso_docket_scan.py, the union rule): THE LINK ROWS — 473 segments in 42 works of the local
  shelf's Babylonian Talmud, Mishnah and Tosefta exports cite a verse of 4:21-7:89 (Sotah 112, Nazir 104, Menachot 32,
  Pesachim 22, Bava Kamma 18, Zevachim 15, Shevuot 12, Yevamot 12, Niddah 11, Kiddushin 10, Keritot 9, Sanhedrin 9, Chullin 8,
  Megillah 8, Mishnah Sotah 8, Ketubot 7, Nedarim 7, Berakhot 6, Taanit 6, Arakhin 5, Mishnah Nazir 5, Temurah 5, Yoma 5, the
  rest under five); THE TOPIC ROWS BY ADDRESS — the Mishnah's rows the seven ledgers' TESTING SHELF lines name (Sotah 1-6 whole
  with 7:1, 7:6, 9:9; Nazir 1-9 whole; Bava Kamma 9:5-12; Nedarim 1:1; Niddah 5:6; Kinnim 1:1, 2:5; Eduyot 5:6; Parah 1:3, 6:1;
  Tamid 7:2; Megillah 4:3-7; Terumot 4:5; Maaser Sheni 5:1-5; Kelim 1:5-9; Sanhedrin 6:2; Makkot 3; Tahorot 4-6), 165 after the
  link duplicates drop — 638 rows in all (Bamidbar's docket was 159). THE LONG TALMUD RANGES the ledgers name ("the tractate is
  the exam") enter through the link rows — Sotah 2a-31a (994 segments, 98 of them link rows), Nazir 2a-66b (1,297 / 104), Bava
  Kamma 103a-111a (274 / 15), Sotah 37b-40a (84 / 6), Shevuot 35b-36a (56 / 4), Menachot 59a-b (52 / 1), Pesachim 67a-68a (48
  / 4), Sotah 28a-29a (45 / 14), Menachot 90b-91a (43 / 1), Yoma 3b-4b (40 / 1), Nazir 47b-48b (34 / 9), Nazir 60a-61a (32 / 5),
  Eruvin 83a-b (32 / 0), Berakhot 19b-20a (27 / 1), Nedarim 9b-10a (25 / 2), Kiddushin 27b-28a (21 / 3) — their remainder is
  enumerated by count as OUTSIDE DECLARED SCOPE in the docket's header, never silently narrowed; Bikkurim, Chullin 24a, Bekhorot

THE FUNCTION (cold_run_naso.py — nine cells, the five motions, the honest-pairing guard, effects on every verdict, every token
probed):
- F1 work_count(case, data): 4:21-49 — the four counts by the parser (2,750 / 2,630 / 3,200 = 8,580 = 4:48); the shares against
  chapter 3's houses (32 / 35 / 52 of a hundred — the frame-carriers the largest share); the ages window (thirty to fifty);
  Ithamar's two houses against Eleazar's; "the service of service" (Arakhin 11a: the song); the closing formula's variant at
  4:41; the princes among the counters; the count CLOSES the Kohathites' debit of 1b and the two counts commanded here.
- F2 camp_purity(case, data): 5:1-4 — the three classes (the leper, the zav, the corpse-impure) and the three camps (the
  bamidbar cell CALLED) — the ladder: the leper out of all three, the zav out of two, the corpse-impure out of one (Sifrei 1:3-4,
  1:7; Pesachim 67a-68a the exam); male and female (1:5's dispute); the warning for Num 19:20's punishment (1:1); the run
  doubled (1:8).
- F3 restitution(case, data): 5:5-10 — the confession (Sanhedrin 6:2's row); the principal "at its head" and the fifth (THE
  PARAMETER ROW fifth_base: a fifth of the principal / a quarter added, Bava Metzia 54a — Sifrei 3:1's dispute), the money
  before the ram (Lev 5's cell CALLED); the proselyte's theft to the priests of the watch (Sifrei 2:1, 4:1; Mishnah Bava Kamma
  9:11-12); the priest-thief does not keep it (4:2); THE GIFTS (5:9-10): every man's holy things his — the owner's choice of
  priest, the firstborn's redemption after thirty days (the bamidbar cell CALLED), the tithe Onkelos inserts.
- F4 sotah(case, data): 5:11-31 — the six conditions read one by one; the warning and the secreting (THE PARAMETER ROW
  secreting_minimum: six settings, Sotah 4a — the reading's row); who drinks and who does not (the betrothed, the widow awaiting
  the levir, the minor's wife — Mishnah Sotah 4); the husband's own gate (5:31 — the waters test only a clean husband, Sotah
  28a; Sifrei 21:3); the offering (barley; NO oil, NO frankincense — the minchah engine CALLED for the sinner's parallel and the
  scoop; a tenth of an ephah; Onkelos' three se'ah for the ephah); the holy water (the laver), the earthen vessel, the floor's
  dust; the hair uncovered within limits; the oath in any language, opened for merit (the row merit_suspends: three, nine,
  twelve months, or not at all — Sifrei 8:1 with R. Shimon; Mishnah Sotah 3:4-5); the scroll written and erased — THE ORDER
  (THE PARAMETER ROW sotah_order: erased-then-drinks / R. Shimon's offering first — Mishnah Sotah 3:2, Sifrei 18:1); the two
  outcomes (guilty: the belly and the thigh, the adulterer too — Sifrei 15:1; innocent: cleared and sown with seed); the
  abolition (Mishnah Sotah 9:9 — Rabban Yochanan ben Zakkai: THE DATA ROW waters_abolished, the tape's setting running).
- F5 nazirite(case, data): 6:1-21 — the vow and its handles (Mishnah Nazir 1); the default term (THE DATA ROW nazir_default_days
  = 30: Sifrei 25:1's derivation from "holy shall he BE", the data channel labeled); women yes, minors no, the father and the
  husband annul; the three prohibitions — the vine's products from kernels to skin (an olive's bulk; combination; the taste as
  the substance — Pesachim 44b), the razor (thirty days' growth), the dead (kin no, the unburied yes — Sifrei 26:1; Berakhot
  19b); the count voided by tumah (all; the ninety-ninth voids all, the hundredth thirty — Sifrei 31:1; Mishnah Nazir 3:3-4)
  and by shaving (thirty); the defiled nazirite's week: the seventh's sprinkling, the eighth's two birds and the lamb (Sifrei
  28:2-30:1; the term restarts — THE TIMER on the exam's scene: the vow sets the due, a defilement cancels and re-sets it); the
  completion (6:13-20): the three animals with the basket, the shaving at the door of the peace offering — where he cooks
  (Sifrei 33:1-34:1), the hair under the pot, the shoulder with a cake and a wafer on his palms and the breast and thigh (Lev
  7's cell CALLED — I11 stated on 6:20); the leper-nazirite's refusals (Sifrei 38:1; Nazir 60a-61a); the libations for the
  vowed (Menachot 90b — Sifrei 37:1).
- F6 blessing(case, data): 6:22-27 — the form table read off "thus" and "say to them" (Sifrei 39:1; Sotah 37b-38b): the holy
  tongue, standing, the raised hands, the Name in the Temple and the epithet in the province (Mishnah Sotah 7:6, Tamid 7:2),
  face to face, aloud, the prompter; who blesses — the blemished-hands priest not (Megillah 24b, Mishnah Megillah 4:7 — the
  priesthood's blemish cell CALLED), the drunk not (Taanit 26b), the four daily times; THE PARAMETER ROW face_lifted (6:26
  against Deut 10:17 — when Israel do His will / when not; the eight pairs); "and I will bless them" (the priests too — Chullin
  49a).
- F7 wagons(case, data): 7:1-9 — the day (the day-stack); the numbers by the parser AFTER THE FIX (6 wagons, 12 oxen; a wagon
  for two princes, an ox for one — 7:3 [6, 12, 2, 1]); "covered" (Rebbi — Onkelos' word); accepted only when told (7:4-5);
  the distribution AS MOSES SAW FIT (7:6-9: two and four to Gershon, four and eight to Merari, Kohath NONE — on the shoulder;
  Sifrei 46:1-2, David's error the flow reversed).
- F8 dedication(case, data): 7:10-88 — brought on the day of the anointing (7:10; 7:84 = 7:88, the same day — Sifrei 53:1);
  the order BY THE JOURNEYING = the camp's order (2:3-31 = 7:12-83, computed), not by birth; one prince per day; the twelve as
  one text (computed); the dish 130 and the bowl 70 by the sanctuary shekel (the shekel cell CALLED — Onkelos' sela), the pan
  TEN of gold weighed in silver (M-26; Sifrei 49:1, 55:1 — 7:86's 120 decides); the animals — one bull, one ram, one lamb for
  the burnt offering, one goat for the sin offering (the grave of the depths — Sifrei 51:1; Horayot 6a), two oxen and five,
  five, five for the peace offering; the totals as checkpoints (12 × the block); THE PRINCE'S FOUR EXCEPTIONS (Sifrei 51:1;
  Menachot 50a; Moed Katan 9a — a private offering on the Sabbath, an individual's incense, a sin offering not for a sin, one
  of each kind); Temple vessels weigh the same singly and together (54:1).
- F9 voice(case, data): 7:89 — the Voice speaking ITSELF (the reflexive by the points; Onkelos' two reflexives); from between
  the cherubim — Lev 1:1 against Exod 25:22 reconciled by 7:89 (I13 stated here, Sifrei 58:1); the thirteen utterances to Moses
  and Aaron and the thirteen exclusions of Aaron (counted against the frame census — measured at the code); the great voice,
  not low (58:2); the Voice at the door (Yoma 4b; Sukkah 5a).
THE DATA ROWS (motion 2, the settings recorded): fifth_base; secreting_minimum; merit_suspends; sotah_order; waters_abolished;
nazir_default_days; nazir_wine_pair (yayin / shekhar: two tongues, diluted and undiluted, or new and old — Onkelos' third);
face_lifted; blessing_name_by_place; princes_order (the camp's, computed — a row for the exam's "not by birth"); wagons_
distribution (the ink's numbers); prince_exceptions; the_twelve_are_one_text (computed at import).

THE DAEMON law_naso (the 49th; file cold_run_naso.py; wraps naso; given_at Num 4:21; installed_by boot — the standing setting).
THE KINDS on the tape (in the registry's two forms): SPEECH — gershon_service_commanded (4:21-28), merari_service_commanded
(4:29-33), send_out_commanded (5:1-3), wagons_accepted_commanded (7:4-5), dedication_order_commanded (7:11); ACTS —
levites_work_counted (4:34-49: the four counts as data fields; CLOSES three debits — the Kohathites' service of 1b and the two

outside_the_camp, wagons_assigned, dedication_brought, dedication_offered, tested_by_the_waters, ketubah_forfeited, nazirite_
vow_bound, nazirite_term_fulfilled, count_voided, blessed_by_the_priests, spoken_to_from_the_ark; reused: commanded, counted,
guilt_acknowledged, adds_fifth, due_to_priest, pays, exempt, put_to_death, disqualified, accepted, not_accepted, charge_kept):
each SPEECH writes `commanded` on its addressee; each ACT writes the status and closes its debit; THE SCHEDULE — the command at
7:11 writes twelve `dedication_offered` effects with dues at the anointing day + (n − 1), each a RETRO-WRITE inside the stretch,
the twelfth closing the body's `dedication_brought`. THE ENTITIES (fourteen, registered before the code): the_princes_of_israel,
the twelve princes, the_unclean_of_the_camp. THE EDGES: naso -> bamidbar CALL reference (the three camps, the thirty days, the
houses), naso -> vayikra5 CALL reference (the fifth and the ram), naso -> minchah CALL reference (the sinner's adjuncts, the
scoop), naso -> tzav CALL reference (the breast and thigh), naso -> priesthood CALL reference (the blemish), naso -> metzora
CALL reference (the shaving), naso -> incense_shekel CALL reference (the sanctuary shekel; the anointing), naso -> offerings
CALL reference (the animals), naso -> negaim / clocks REFERENCE (the leper's and the zav's statuses), naso -> (the heifer) OWED
FORWARD (the corpse-impure — with 8:7's water), sequence -> naso CALL none; bamidbar -> minchah stays OWED (4:16's continual
meal-offering waits for Num 28 — the sotah's fistful is naso's own edge).

THE TAPE (the stitcher): thirteen retrograde markers — mk('Num 7:1', 'R', [], "M['naso_dedication'] = M['erected']; w.marker(...)")
and the twelve day-heads (7:12-66 by ordinals [1] ... [10]; 7:72 [11]; 7:78 [12]) at M['erected'] + (n − 1); the eleven lines by
the register test (five speeches within their frames, six acts on narrative verbs); 4:34-49 and 5:4 page-order inside 1:1's

a miss reported never repaired): CD1 the four work-counts summed = 4:48 (8,580); CD2 the wagons 6 = 2 + 4 and the oxen 12 = 4 + 8
(7:3 against 7:7-8, Kohath none); CD3 the totals 7:84-88 = 12 × Nahshon's block (2,400 = 12 × (130 + 70); 120 = 12 × 10;
12 / 12 / 12 / 12; 24 / 60 / 60 / 60); CD4 the twelve days' princes = the camp's order (2:3-31); CD5 the 7:1 marker RETROGRADE
at the erection's day and the twelve day-heads at erected + (n − 1); CD6 the twelve retro-writes' dues = the day-heads' stated
days (the schedule against the stamps); CD7 the four closes (the Kohathites' service, the two counts, the dedication);
CD8 the thirteen exclusions of Aaron against the frame census (the number computed at the code). THE PREDICTION (typed from the
stitcher's print and the code's own arithmetic BEFORE the run): events +11, markers +13 (F 115 unchanged; R 3 -> 16), timers
set unchanged, fired unchanged, RETRO-WRITES +12 (0 -> 12), writes +N (the code's count), daemons fired +1, entities +14, the
four pairs unchanged, closes +4. THE REST: NEWEST_RUNNER = 'naso', PREVIOUS_RUN = 1b's RUN exactly (the tape minus the eleven
lines and the thirteen markers inside the declared span [[Num,4,21,49],[Num,5,1,31],[Num,6,1,27],[Num,7,1,89]] reproduces it).

the indexes regenerated, the state doc, THE_STEPS, THE_BRIEFING, RESUME, memory).

THE DAEMON'S FORM — A LESSON: the first daemon returned the cells' effect lists through a variable (E_(x, ...) for x in e), and the
daemon gate's parser, which reads LITERAL effect names per kind, parsed nothing for the eight case kinds (declared != parsed, eight
fails). Rewritten as every earlier runner writes them: each case kind names LITERALLY every effect it can write (a dict of literal
E_ calls; the cell's list selects from it), so an effect a cell returns that the daemon did not name is a KeyError — a miss to read,
never a silent write. It caught one on its first run: the gifts kind can write PAYS (the firstborn's five shekels after thirty days),
which the declaration lacked — added to both. The declarations corrected from the cells' own written effects: sotah_case writes
put_to_death (never not_accepted), nazirite_case writes released (never karet_cut_off), dedication_case has no exempt row,
theft_confessed_case writes exempt (the robber who died). The daemon gate: 49 daemons, 347 functions WRAPPED, satisfied.

THE DEPENDENCY GATE fired as designed and was answered on file: naso -> chatat VIA offerings (6:11, 6:14, 6:16, 7:16 the sin-offering
proper reached through the offerings engine's dispatch; 5:6-7's "sins" the noun, the erection row's homograph); naso -> family FALSE
(5:21 "to the woman" — the dative, not the marriage formula); naso -> lev24 FALSE (5:19, 5:20, 5:29 "UNDER your husband", 6:18 the fire
UNDER the pot — the preposition four times); naso -> pesach FALSE (6:17 the basket of unleavened bread — the nazirite's loaves, Lev
2:4's forms, the incense_shekel row's precedent); the pointer Num 5:4 AS_WHEN RUN_CITATION (the run line citing 5:2-3's command, the
verb SPOKE not "commanded" — the census's form catches both); and the registration edge sequence -> naso live once the runner's
import line joined cold_run_sequence.py (the tape's daemons() reads sys.modules — the first tape run raised KeyError 'cold_run_naso'
with the DAEMON_ORDER row in place and no import: the import line is the live edge, the row alone is not).

## Sitting 3b — THE COMPILE OF BEHA'ALOTCHA, Numbers 8:1-26 + 10:1-12:16 (the design, 2026-09-10; the owner: "Keep going" after 2b's
## close, on the ruling READ THEN COMPILE; written before the docket, the types and the code, on 1b's order)

THE MEASUREMENTS (computed 2026-09-10 before this paragraph was typed):
- THE PARSER'S STATE at the portion's seats after 2b's rules: 10:2 [2] and 11:26 [2] — the construct "two of" PAID by 2b; 8:2 [7], 8:24
  [25], 10:4 [1], 10:11 [20], 10:33 [3, 3], 11:32 [10], 12:14 [7, 7] right; THE GAPS: 11:19 [1, 5, 10, 20] (the dual "two days" silent),
  11:31 [] ("about two cubits"), 12:4 [] ("the three of you... the three of them"), 10:36 [] (two plural nouns, no numeral — the shelf's
  22,000 is a DATUM, not a parse).
- THE DUAL-NOUN CLASS measured on the whole Tanakh DB (a script over every word, consonants only): the dual of a UNIT NOUN carries the
  numeral two in its ending — יומים ("two days") 3 Torah seats (Exod 16:29, 21:21, Num 11:19); אמתים ("two cubits") 9 (Exod 25:10, 25:17,
  25:23, 30:2, 37:1, 37:6, 37:10, 37:25, Num 11:31); שנתים ("two years") 3 — already in UNITS; מאתים ("two hundred") 18 and אלפים ("two
  thousand" / "thousands") 27 — already read. NOT numerals and left as words: שבעתים ("sevenfold", Gen 4:15, 4:24 — an adverb), פעמים
  ("times" — the noun of occurrences after a numeral, "seven times"; bare "twice" at Gen 27:36 is the one seat of that sense), חדשים
  ("months", plural).
- THE SUFFIXED-NUMERAL CLASS: a numeral with a PRONOUN suffix counts the persons — שניהם ("the two of them") 35 Torah seats, שניכם ("the two
  of you", Gen 27:45), שנינו ("the two of us", Gen 31:37), שלשתכם ("the three of you") and שלשתם ("the three of them") both at Num 12:4 — 39
  tokens in 38 Torah verses; the Tanakh adds ארבעתם ("the four of them", 7), שבעתם / שבעתך (3 + 3), שתיהם / שתיהן (5 + 2), שלשתם (2 more).
  The plain שנים / שתים ("two", the ם the dual ending, not "them") are UNITS already and are NOT this class.
- THE HALF: וחצי ("and a half") immediately after a NUMERAL continues the number by a half — Exod 25:10, 25:17, 37:1, 37:6 ("two cubits and
  a half"); the same word after the bare unit noun ("a cubit and a half", 25:10b, 25:17b, 26:16, 37:1b) has no numeral before it and stays
  silent — THE SINGULAR UNIT NOUN AS ONE is a class this sitting names and does not read (the residue, recorded).
- THE DAY-STACK ON THE SHELF: Taanit 29a:2-5 (the Babylonian Talmud, the exam's bridge) lays the portion's dates end to end — Exod 40:17 the
  erection (2, 1, 1); Num 10:11 the twentieth of Iyar; 10:33 "three days' journey" ("adds to the first twenty days an additional three days"
  — 29a:3); the month of flesh (11:20); Hazeroth, Miriam's seven days, "they remained in Hazeroth until the twenty-ninth of Sivan" (29a:4);
  the baraita "on the twenty-ninth of Sivan Moses sent the spies" and the return on the Ninth of Av with the gemara's own "forty days minus
  one" (29a:5). Seder Olam Rabbah 8:2 (on the shelf, Midrash/Aggadah) tells the same stack with THE TWENTY-EIGHTH of Sivan for the spies
  — the shelf disagreeing with itself by a day: a parameter row (spies_sent_day: 29 Taanit 29a:5 the running setting, the exam's shelf; 28
  Seder Olam 8:2). The spies' own marker belongs to Shelach's sitting; THIS portion takes three READING-PLACED FORWARD markers from Taanit
  29a: Num 11:1 at (2, 2, 23) — the three days' journey elapsed; Num 11:35 at (2, 3, 22) — the arrival at Hazeroth, the twenty-ninth less the
  seven days stated at 29a:4; Num 12:16 at (2, 3, 29) — the journey to Paran on the day the spies go. The ink's own date 10:11 (2, 2, 20) is
  the tape's FORWARD text-constrained marker (sitting 3's measurement).
- THE CALENDAR: the running setting rounds the months 30 / 29 (calendar_parameters month_rounding; the rabbis' "Iyar of that year was made
  full" is year 1's arm, UNEXERCISED). Year 2's Iyar is therefore 29 days on the machine: (2, 2, 23) + 30 = (2, 3, 24); the tradition's
  "a month" counted INCLUSIVELY from the twenty-third with a full Iyar lands on the twenty-second — THE MONTH TIMER WILL DIVERGE FROM THE
  HAZEROTH MARKER BY TWO DAYS, expected and filed (the same inclusive-count problem the gemara names at 29a:5 as "forty days minus one").

THE DAEMON law_beha: the spec/run pairs as debits closed by their runs — lamps_commanded → lamps_lit closes it; levites_purification_commanded
→ levites_purified_and_given closes it; trumpets_commanded → a debit left OPEN in this span (its run is Num 31:6 — the readback names it);
elders_commanded → elders_prophesied closes the gathering and SETS the month timer (flesh_for_a_month, due = the speech's day + 30);
ark_journeyed SETS the three-days timer (journey_of_three_days, due + 3); miriam_stricken_and_shut_out SETS the seven-days timer
(confined_seven_days on miriam, due + 7 — the negaim effect reused: the same verb) and writes the halt (journey_halted, a block on israel —
a NEW effect if the registry holds no halt of a journey: measured at the types) closed by paran_reached; quail_and_plague writes put_to_death
(HEAVEN) on the-lusters and the graves as the place; fire_of_the_lord_burned writes the fire's status and its sinking at Moses' prayer;
march_in_order writes arrayed_by_banners (Bamidbar's effect) with the order as value and the tabernacle's journeying; hobab_asked writes the
plea as a status with no close (Judg 1:16 the readback); miriam_spoke writes the speech's status; levite_age_rule installs the parameter
(rule_installed? no — a statute's effect: the age as a DATA row, the ledger carries commanded on the-levites with the value 'the_age_rule',
closed by nothing in the span — Chronicles' re-setting is the readback's). The case kinds dispatch to the cells with LITERAL effects (2b's
form). Effects reused: commanded, accepted, disqualified, exempt, appointed_to_serve, arrayed_by_banners, lamp_arranged, confined_seven_days,
put_to_death, waved, given_to_aaron, prophesied? (measured at the types — a new effect 'spirit_rested' for 11:25-26 if none), blessed?;

(status), sent_out_seven_days? (no — confined_seven_days reused).

  carrying at least one (measured line by line); chapter 15's two register verses are its frames (15:1, 15:17) — no tape line.

THE FUNCTION (cold_run_shelach.py — the 46th runner; law_shelach the 51st daemon; the cells answer the docket's rows):

## Sitting 4b — AS BUILT (2026-09-10; the order above run end to end)

THE DOCKET (logic/oral_triage/num_13_15_shelach_exam_2026-09-10.md, 313 rows — 180 link rows in 37 works, 133 topic rows by address after
the link-duplicates; LAW 141, DERIVATION 45, DISPUTE 31, CONTEXT 96; the 31 long ranges sized in the header, read from the scan's own print;
gloss lint 0 after three flags read — a hyphenated Hebrew-derived compound and an unglossed "tanna"). Its crowns: THE FORTY MINUS ONE (Taanit
29a:4-7 — the gemara counts thirty-nine itself and Abaye makes Tammuz full); THE DEFINITE ONE ON THE ANSWER SHEET (Menachot 91b:9, 91b:20
— the tradition reads the very token the parser had left silent); THE TABLE IN LOGS (Mishnah Menachot 9:2-4, 12:4, 13:5 — the hin's twelve,
the 3-4-6 floors, the Sukkot-Sabbath sixty-one); THE CENSUS SET WITH ITS EDGES (Bava Batra 121b:8-11, 121a:9 — Levi outside, the over-sixty by
the valuations' "and upward", the dying ceased on the fifteenth of Av); THE CONGREGATION OF TEN (Mishnah Sanhedrin 1:6 with two verbal
analogies); THE WHOLE TORAH JUXTAPOSED TO IDOLATRY (Horayot 8a:14; Mishnah Horayot 2:6's table = chatat.rank's own collapse); THE ALEPH ON
THE ANSWER SHEET (Horayot 13a:4 — sitting 4's crown as the gemara's ground); THE FORK ON THE DOUBLED INFINITIVE at three seats; THE
CONVERT'S ENTRY; THE LAND GATE'S TWO ARMS; ZELOPHEHAD AMONG THE PRESUMERS (Shabbat 97a:1 — THE TENT's fourth case reaching back). Eight
entries in MIDDOT.md.

THE PARSER: census_probes rows F1-F33 and O4 typed to FAIL (34 of 34 failed as designed), then the rules built in cold_run_sequence.py's INK
block — (12) THE FRACTION before a measure noun (the fraction words emitted with a percent sign when the next word is the hin, the ephah,
the shekel, the kab or the homer; exact Fractions); (13) THE UNIT NOUN AS ONE (the tenth, the cubit by its points, the hin — an at sign; a
numeral before it closes, a numeral adjective within two words silences it, the doubled tenth the distributive); (14) THE DEFINITE ONE
(the numeral with the hash — it closes its phrase; before a conjoined numeral it joins: F34 Exod 12:18 = [14, 21], read off the diff);
(15) the third-generation homograph by the lamed's vowel and Sheshai by the tsere; (16) the tithe verb by the a-vowel (patach) or the silent mark (sheva) under the
ayin. THE CORPUS-WIDE DIFF (shelach_parser_diff.py, the 3b tool with the 3b parser as its base) read verse by verse: 110 moved — the
definite one at seventy-odd seats (the boards, the curtains, the lambs, the names), the fractions and unit nouns at their seats, the
cubits (Exod 25:10 [2.5, 1.5, 1.5]; 30:2 [1, 1, 2]), the five third-generation seats and Gen 6:16 dropped from thirty, the three tithe
verbs from ten, Sheshai from the sixth, and TWO MORE the hand had not listed: Num 2:24's "and they journey THIRD" (the hiriq's class,
read as thirty since 1b) and Exod 12:18's "the one and twentieth" (the join rule). Old rows retyped from the reading: N10 Exod 38:26
(the half-shekel now a fraction), D4-D6 (the cubit as one — 3b's residue paid), R8 Num 7:85 and cold_run_naso's TOTALS and CD3 (the one
dish, the one bowl). 110/110 after. RESEARCH_LOG 2026-09-10 (the fourth entry) carries the classes.

THE RUNNER (World/step9/cold_run_shelach.py, written in five parts and joined): 86 probes; the numbers asserted at import — the parser's

WINDOW RUNS FORWARD FROM EACH HEBREW WORD — a twelve-word cut flags its head even when glossed at its tail; split long cuts into glossed
pieces. THE SENTENCE-FINAL CUT PATCHED BY SCRIPT AT SCALE — 180 unglossed cuts glossed in one pass by the Hebrew gloss that precedes each
(the literal renderings; the divergent ones were glossed by hand already). THE RITUAL RETYPES THE TRUTH'S LITERALS ITSELF (the ALL_UNITS
proof step) — the hand's edit found them moved; run CORPUS_TRUTH, do not retype it. THE SHELF'S ROW CAN STOP MID-TEXT (the translator's
gap) — read to the last rendered word and name the gap in the ledger and the claim. THE SHELF'S CITATIONS INSIDE THE ROWS ARE MISTYPED
TOO (three this sitting) — read the quotation to its verse, as the heads are placed by position. THE MEASUREMENT PASS BEFORE THE ASSERTS,
AGAIN — nine failures on the first typed pass even so. THE PARSER'S NEW GAP SITS ON THE PORTION'S OWN NUMBER (the 250 with its article at
16:35, right without it at 16:2 and 16:17): measure the portion's every numeral verse, not the new ones only.

  THE BEST OF THE OIL, WINE AND GRAIN (18:12 — terumah gedolah: NO MEASURE IN THE INK, Mishnah Terumot 4:3's 1/40, 1/50, 1/60 the data
  channel's, naso's row CALLED — 15:20'S POINTER PAID: the challah cell's as_terumah ask → this cell's terumah ask; the five border terms
  on 18:12's clauses), bikkurim "holy while attached" (18:13), EVERY DEVOTED THING (18:14 — gentiles', women's and bondsmen's too; THE
  FOUR-WAY DISPUTE on unqualified devotions: R. Yossi HaGelili — the priests; R. Yehudah b. Beteira — Temple maintenance; R. Yehudah b.
  Bava — the priests; R. Shimon — Heaven: four authorities on one pair of verses (Lev 27:21 / 27:28) — temurah.devote CALLED for the
  Sages / R. Yehuda b. Beteira arm, the DATA row devotion_default carrying the four), THE FIRSTBORN'S REDEMPTION (18:15-18 — bamidbar.levites
  CALLED: from a month = 3:15's threshold; five shekels, twenty gerah "more, not less" (118:1; the shekel engine's seat asserted); the
  ass alone, with a sheep — pesach CALLED; immediately (the ass) or after a month (the man); the money's class — movable, not bondsmen,
  writs or land (Rebbi: writs only) — DATA; "ox, sheep, goat — no hybrid"; THE CLEAN FIRSTBORN NOT REDEEMED (18:17), its blood ONE
  APPLICATION (Mishnah Zevachim 5:8; 118:1's "they are consecrated" — the tithe and the paschal lamb take one spilling: Yoshiyah / the fats:
  Yitzchak — a DISPUTE row), its flesh "as the wave-breast and the right thigh" (18:18 — TWO DAYS AND A NIGHT, Zevachim 5:8 — the
  offerings engine's shelamim row CALLED for the analogy), the blemished firstborn to the priest (Bekhorot 4-5's rows as test data with
  Deut 15:21-22 named as the run outside this span)), A COVENANT OF SALT (18:19 — Aaron's and David's, 2 Chr 13:5 — Sifrei 119:5: Aaron's
  covenant greater than David's, for the wicked sons too; Avot 4:13's three crowns, 119:4).
- F5 the_tithe (18:20-32): NO INHERITANCE (18:20, 18:23-24 — inheritance_barred on Aaron and the Levites; THE EXCLUSION TABLE FROM 26:53
  as DATA: priests (18:20), Levites (18:23), bondsmen and proselytes (26:55), the two of uncertain sex (26:54) — Sifrei 119:1; the
  Zelophehad engine's exclusion by sin CALLED beside it; "I am your portion" → Onkelos "the gifts I have given you"), THE TITHE TO THE
  LEVITES (18:21 "in exchange for their service" — THE WAGE CONDITIONED ON SERVICE: "if he serves he takes", a Levite who refused one
  service has no portion (119:5, 122:1); "the Levite — he" = perforce, in sabbatical and jubilee years too, no priest in his stead (R.
  Nathan's a-fortiori refused); Yevamot 86a-b: the tithe given to the priests by Ezra's penalty — DATA tithe_recipient {levite: the ink;
  priest: Ezra's penalty}), the Israelites' approach (18:22 "lest they bear sin and die"), the Levites bear their own iniquity (18:23),
  THE TITHE OF THE TITHE (18:26 — 1/10 × 1/10 = 1/100 by arithmetic, Fraction; Neh 10:39's run; "from it" — kind for its kind, rooted for
  rooted, new for new, not across the border — Terumot 1:5's rows; "from it" MUFNEH for the mourner — the tithe and the paschal lamb,
  Pesachim 36a / Yevamot 73b-74a), RECKONED AS THE GRAIN OF THE THRESHING FLOOR (18:27 — 15:20's pointer paid; only if taken as
  prescribed — R. Yishmael; the terumah of the tithe likened to terumah gedolah and the likeness returning — both obligatory; BY ESTIMATE
  AND BY THOUGHT; from what is processed — THE THRESHOLDS: the pile evened, the wine skimmed, the oil dripped (Mishnah Ma'aserot 1:5-8 —
  DATA tithe_thresholds); "so you too" — the priests separate too; the three a-fortioris), FROM ALL ITS BEST (18:29-30 — a warning to take
  the choicest, Onkelos "its beauty"; NEUTRALIZED IN A HUNDRED AND ONE — holiness_b's ratio CALLED, the translator's gap at 121:1 named),
  WHAT REMAINS IS COMMON (18:30 — as the floor's grain after terumah), "IN EVERY PLACE" (18:31 — even a cemetery, the a-fortiori from
  terumah refused; Deut 12:13's ban the Torah twin), "you and your household" (18:31 — the Israelite wife of a Levite may depute), "it is
  your WAGE" (18:31), "you shall not profane... and you shall not die" (18:32 — the warning to Levites and Israelites both; the priests'

THE DOCKET (logic/oral_triage/num_16_18_korach_exam_2026-09-10.md, 316 rows — 209 link rows in 36 works, 107 topic rows by address; LAW
195, DERIVATION 46, DISPUTE 42, CONTEXT 33; 94 CREDITED prior reads with their ledgers named on the row, each given its quick look; the 33
long ranges sized from the scan's own print; gloss lint 0 after one flag read — a hyphenated compound). Its crowns: KORACH'S DEATH ARGUED
ON THE SHELF (Sanhedrin 110a:13-14 — R. Yochanan reads 16:32 as NOT Korach and 26:10 as not him either, the plague; the baraita (the outside
teaching) both burned and swallowed: the ink's silence at 16:32 is the tradition's own open question); THE TRANSLATION LOAD-BEARING AGAIN
(Zevachim 91a:19 cites Onkelos' "for greatness" by name as the ground for how the priests eat); THE DECREE OVER THE A-FORTIORI ON THE ANSWER
SHEET (Mishnah Ketubot 5:3's "a court that convened after them said: not until she enters the canopy" against Sifrei 117:2's argument);
THE STRANGER'S DEATH BY TWO IDENTITIES (Sanhedrin 84a:13; Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6 — the row zar_who_served already Bamidbar's at 1:51, read by
CALL); THE TITHE OF THE TITHE ONE IN A HUNDRED with THE LEVITE WHO PRECEDED THE PRIEST at six seats (on the stalks the tithe's terumah only,
after the pile the great terumah too); BY ESTIMATE AND BY THOUGHT (Abba Elazar ben Gomel's plural "your terumah" at five Talmud seats — Sifrei
121:1's own row); THE COAL PANS ELEVATED (Menachot 99a:11 — "one elevates in sanctity" derived from 17:3); THE COURT'S SUMMONS READ OFF THE
CHAPTER (Moed Katan 16a:3-5); THE FIRSTBORN'S WINDOW BY JUXTAPOSITION (two days and a night; one placement on the base); THE STAFF BESIDE
THE ARK ("for safekeeping" / "for safekeeping"); A CONGREGATION IS TEN, FROM KORACH. One correction APPENDED to the docket after the runner's
first import: the terumah's measure ROW is this runner's own (Shelach carried a placeholder copy marked OWED); the naso engine's cell is the
floor — the docket's "naso's row CALLED" notes read that way.

  ink's thirty-eight years (Deut 2:14) end on the decree's own day by the Calendar, the Zered crossed after the mourning by the text's

  rise up, O well, answer it: the well the princes dug, the nobles of the people delved, with the lawgiver, with their staffs" — the
  sea's "then sang" (Exod 15:1) and Miriam's "answer" (15:21) at their second seats; the LAWGIVER Judah's word (Gen 49:10 — Onkelos

  Nedarim 55a / Eruvin 54a: Rava's reading of Mattanah, Nahaliel, Bamoth — the Torah as the well's gift: from the wilderness a gift; the

  field of Moab, the top of Pisgah, looking over the wasteland" — Moses' death-view Deut 34:1, 3:27; Balaam's 23:14), SIHON (21:21-22 the
  messengers — "let me pass through your land; we will not turn into field or vineyard, we will not drink the water of the well; by the

  2:26-29 Kedemoth; Judg 11:19 — plea_made toward Sihon; 21:23 "and Sihon did not let Israel pass... and gathered all his people and went

  reason, Judg 11:20's distrust; 21:24-25 "and Israel smote him with the edge of the sword and possessed his land from Arnon to Jabbok,
  as far as the sons of Ammon, for the border of the sons of Ammon was strong; and Israel took all these cities and dwelt in all the
  cities of the Amorite, in Heshbon and all its daughters" — kings_smitten on Sihon; land_possessed on Israel; Jabbok Jacob's ford;

  through Sihon" — Israel's title to Moab's land by Sihon's conquest of it: the row sihon_purified — THE ANSWER SHEET for the parable),
  THE PARABLE (21:26-30 "for Heshbon was the city of Sihon king of the Amorite, who had fought against the former king of Moab and taken
  all his land out of his hand as far as Arnon; therefore the parable-tellers say: come to Heshbon... for a fire went out from Heshbon, a

THE DATA (parameter rows, the tradition's own vocabulary; the running setting first — the docket fixes the arms): heifer_age (Parah 1:1),

ashes), tvul_yom_fit (Parah 3:7 — the Sadducees' row: the burner deliberately defiled and immersed), sprinkle_toward (Parah 3:9 — the
sanctuary's door seen), bundle (Parah 3:10-11 — the cedar, hyssop and scarlet cast as one), ashes_thirds (Parah 3:11 — the rampart, the
Mount of Olives, the watches), nine_heifers (Parah 3:5 — Moses', Ezra's, seven after: R. Meir / the Sages), meilah_on_the_heifer (Menachot
51b-52a), removes (Oholot 1:1-3 — the corpse's grades: seven days / until evening / a father / a father of fathers), tent_measure (Oholot
3:6-7 — a handbreadth), bone_measure (Oholot 2:1-3 — a barley-grain; the quarter-log; the limb; the spine and skull), vessel_census (Sifrei
126 — six classes by three verses; Kelim 1:1-4 the fathers of impurity), tzamid_patil (Kelim 9:2-3, 10:1-2 — the lid's materials; Onkelos
"of earthenware"), living_water (Parah 8:8-11 — the springs fit and unfit; the sea's water), mixing_order (Sifrei 128 / Sotah 16b — the
ashes on the water; Parah 6:1 the order reversed unfit; naso's dust_order the sotah's), third_day_fixed (Sifrei 125 — the third fixed, the
seventh counted; Yoma 8a), punishment_split (Sifrei 125-126 — uncleanness for the omission, karet for the entry), sprinkler_measure (Sifrei
129 — enough to dip the hyssop's tips; Parah 12:1-2), sprinkler_or_carrier (Sifrei 130 / Parah 12:5 — the sprinkler clean, the carrier
unclean), a_clean_man (Sifrei 124, 129 — the woman excluded / a child; Parah 5:4, 12:10), miriam_death_day {tenth_of_nisan: Seder Olam 10:2;
first_of_nisan: Seder Olam 9:2's new moon — the arrival's}, well_by_merit (Taanit 9a — Miriam's; returned by Moses' and Aaron's), death_by
_the_kiss (Moed Katan 28a — Miriam; Bava Batra 17a — Aaron and Moses: the six), meribah_sin (the docket's arms), edom_passage (the ink /
Deut 2:28-29), moserah (Seder Olam 9:2 — the seven stations' retreat), succession_by (the garments / the anointing — Horayot 12a, Yoma 12b),
aaron_mourned_by_all (Avot de-Rabbi Natan 12 — the docket), arad_is_amalek (Rosh Hashanah 3a), arad_heard (Rosh Hashanah 3a — the clouds

(2 Kgs 18:4; Pesachim 56a — the sages agreed), serpent_kills_or_heals (Mishnah Rosh Hashanah 3:8 — the heart subjected), arnon_miracle
(Berakhot 54a-b), well_song_reading (Nedarim 55a / Eruvin 54a — Rava), parable_tellers (Chullin 60b — Balaam and Beor), sihon_purified
(Chullin 60b / Gittin 38a — Ammon and Moab purified through Sihon), ammon_border (the ink's STRONG / Deut 2:19's COMMANDED), og_lore
(Berakhot 54b; Niddah 24b, 61a), spy_verb (Josh 14:7 Caleb's word). Every row's value the tradition's; the docket may add or strike arms.

the hyssop), yoma CALL (the finger's seat), chatat CALL (the carcass list, the seven sprinklings, the blood inside), vayikra5 CALL
