THE POPULATION TABLE — THE DESIGN (the speculation's item 5 built; ARCHITECTURE/DATABASE_SPECULATION.md section 3 answered at the compile):
- WHERE IT LIVES: in the WORLD ENGINE as state — World.tables['population'], a list of rows — because daemons must query it DURING the run
  (the census daemon reads its own chapter-1 rows to write the deltas at 26); the journal indexes it afterward as the fifth view.
- TWO GRAINS, NO INVENTED INDIVIDUALS: `counted` rows keyed by the census (as_of: the event's verse), the TRIBE (the ink's name), the FAMILY
  (None on a tribe's total; the family's name with its gentilic on a family row; a house row for Levi's three carries level=house) and the
  COUNT (the number the ink writes; None where the ink writes none — chapter 26's families carry NO count: the silence stays silent);
  `named` rows for the PERSONS THE ROLL NAMES (person, tribe, family, father, status — every value a clause the ink writes: "died in the land
  of Canaan", "swallowed / consumed", "did not die", "had no sons, only daughters", "born to Levi in Egypt", "when they brought near strange
  fire", "excepted — remained"); `delta` rows DECLARED at the second census from the table's own chapter-1 rows (from_verse, to_verse, delta,
  explained — a list of the tape's explanations by CALL with the tribe-assignment's provenance — and unexplained: the labeled remainder).
- THE SCHEMA IS A REGISTRY: World/step9/population_schema.yaml — the FIFTH registry beside the event, effect, calendar and installation
  registries — the columns the ink's own words with their Hebrew and verse (tribe מַטֶּה "tribe" 26:55 / the "sons of" heads; family מִשְׁפָּחָה
  "family" 26:5 ×78; fathers' house 26:2; "the number of names" 1:2, 26:53; the counted פְּקֻדִים "the counted" 26:51; "son of" 26:1; the
  statuses the clauses above); the grains with their required and optional columns; a row outside the schema is REFUSED.
- THE LAW OF THE TABLE, IN CODE: World.row(table, row) accepts a row ONLY while a daemon is consuming an event (self._consuming set) — a
  row written by hand is refused with SystemExit ("never by hand"); the engine stamps written_by (the daemon), day, year, the table; the
  row is NOT a ledger entry (no op, no open, no close, no counterparty) and moves no count of the RUN tuple; it is the log's own NINTH
  class ROW, journaled as run.row (subj the row's subject through the registry — a named row's person id, a counted row's tribe name; unit
  the daemon; ref the verse). World.population(**where) answers equality queries on the rows — the daemons' instrument.
- THE JOURNAL: KINDS gains ROW → run.row; the register event_kinds.yaml gains run.row; the sink's _append_log handles the class; the FIFTH
  VIEW run_population (source, seq, subject, grain, as_of, tribe, family, level, person, father, status, count, delta, unexplained,
  written_by, verse) in run_views.sql; the views gate's population check (view rows = run.row lines); the FIFTH QUESTION `population
  [tribe]` in the ask-tool; journal_probes J3 counts the classes the probe world exercises (seven) against KINDS less run.skip and run.row.
- WHO WRITES: the census daemon law_second_census — on census_taken (chapter 1's event, a SHARED KIND at its second consumer: the twelve
  tribe rows, no families) and on levites_counted (chapter 3's: the three houses and the Levites' total as written) — rows only, no effect
  (an empty watch); on the second census's lines: the twelve tribe rows, the fifty-seven family rows, the twelve delta rows, the Levites'
  rows and delta, the named rows. Births and deaths already live in the life eras and the ledger; no birth or death row is written here.
- WHO READS (the first consumers, the table's proof): THE DAUGHTERS' ROW — 26:33's five named rows carry "had no sons, only daughters"
  BEFORE the daughters' marker at 27:1 on the tape; the Zelophehad runner's heir_of(daughter only) = daughter answers from the premise
  the roll wrote (by CALL; CP7); JOCHEBED'S ROW — 26:59's "born to Levi in Egypt" is the ink witness the Joseph runner's CJ3b waited on
  (JO.seventy('jochebed') the shelf's answer by CALL); the verdict stays DIVERGE — the ink's own count is unchanged (32 living named): the
  row is the WITNESS, not a resolution (CP8).
- WHAT IT CANNOT DO, BY DESIGN (the speculation's item 7 kept): no family below the count the ink gives (the families carry None); no delta
  resolved the text leaves unexplained (Simeon's 13,100 beyond the plague, the Levites' +1,000, every other tribe's whole delta — labeled
  unexplained); no age for an uncounted person.
- FILED, NOT BUILT (the owner's call on the recommendation): the backward seeding — the ark's kind table (Gen 6:19-20, 7:2-3, 7:14, 8:19),
  the nations table (Gen 10), Genesis 46's roster by name (the Joseph runner's ROSTERS hold it as lists) — a COMPILE_DEBT line naming the
  consumer that would call for it (a daemon querying a person before Numbers 1); the ark's "kinds do not mix" invariant is the tradition's
  reading until Leviticus 19:19 writes it.
- THE LINK QUESTION ON THE ARK: the reading's claim PN26A-12 set 26:63-64's predicate beside the ark's "all that came out" (Gen 9:10) — a
  shared PHRASE, no teacher; under the link review law a compile edge on it would be a TRANSFER without a teacher: NOT a CALL to the primeval
  runner; the runner carries it as ONE HYPOTHESIS cell (class H — the sixth provenance tag, counted apart in the FRACTIONS line), the
  value kept, the flag changed.

block with the two SHARED kinds, the functions block, the span + nine edges, I5 55) -> the gates to FAIL (the daemon gate 2 ways, the
dependency gate 9) -> the runner in one file -> the first graded run -> the gates read -> the recorder -> the stitcher -> the literals ->
the tape run -> the probe gates -> the journal gate -> the sweep -> the records.

THE ENGINE (world_engine.py; the design's table built as typed): the fifth registry loaded beside the four (POP_SCHEMA); World.tables a list
per table; World.row refuses a row written by hand (no daemon consuming) and a row outside the schema (an unknown table, an unknown grain, a
missing required column, an unknown column — the three refusals P3 proves), stamps written_by / day / year / table, appends, logs the class
ROW; World.population answers equality queries. The journal: KINDS gains ROW -> run.row; the sink writes subj (the registry id of a named
row's person, a counted row's tribe name), unit (the daemon), ref (the verse); the fifth view run_population over run.row; the views gate's
sixth check (population = rows); the fifth question. journal_probes J3: the seven engine classes the probe world exercises are KINDS less
run.skip and run.row. Every probe file green: population 9/9, journal 6/6, view 6/6, installation 6/6 (I5 55), cursor 6/6, sequence 4/4, clock 22 PASS.

  FIFTH part" the noun read as five — new). Lev 16:29, 23:27, 25:9 silent with 29:7.
- GAP 23 MEASURED ON THE WHOLE TANAKH — a unit numeral followed by "and" + a unit numeral: twenty-three seats. The year-word seats (2 Sam 2:11,
  5:5; 1 Chr 3:4; Gen 5:7, 5:11, 5:14, 11:13, 11:15 — the parser's starred "years", not units), a name (Beer-sheba, Josh 19:2), the doubled counts
  outside the Torah ("six and six" 1 Chr 20:6 / 2 Sam 21:20, "seven and seven" Zech 4:2), the DEFINITE one before "and" + a unit (Exod 25:32,
  37:18 — rule 18 closes them already; 1 Kgs 6:24, 7:16, 7:17, 7:27; Ezek 1:6, 10:21 — every one under a disjunctive), and the BARE "one" before
  "and" + a unit at TWO Torah seats: Num 28:19 (the etnachta, the mid-verse pause — [2, 8]) and Exod 36:10 "one to one, AND FIVE curtains" (the
  etnachta — read [5, 1, 6, 1, 1] since the parser's first day: THE SAME GAP AT A SECOND SEAT, found by the census). THE JOIN THAT MUST SURVIVE:
  Gen 8:13 "in the ONE and six hundredth year" — "one" under a CONJUNCTIVE (a qadma) before "and six [hundred]" = 601; every compound "one and N"
  of the four books joins under a conjunctive (Exod 12:18 a darga; Num 1:41, 2:16, 2:28, 31:34, 31:39). So the accent-free form ("a unit before
  'and' + a unit is never one number") is REFUSED by Gen 8:13 — the unit multiplies the hundreds there — and THE RULE IS M-26's: (25) a UNIT
  numeral (one to nine) carrying a DISJUNCTIVE accent before "and" + a unit numeral CLOSES its number — the accent read extended from the
  bare-numeral case (2b) and the definite one (5b) to the conjoined case. Predicted moves: Num 28:19 → [2, 1, 7]; Exod 36:10 → [5, 1, 1, 5, 1, 1].
- GAP 24 — עשור ("the tenth", plene, with the vav): sixteen seats in the Tanakh, one word at every one — the tenth-day noun בֶּעָשׂוֹר ("on the
  tenth" of the month) at twelve (Lev 16:29, 23:27, 25:9; Num 29:7; Josh 4:19; 2 Kgs 25:1; Jer 52:4, 52:12; Ezek 20:1, 24:1, 40:1 — each before
  "of the month"), the bare noun at Gen 24:55 ("days or TEN" — a ten of days), the instrument at Ps 33:2, 92:4, 144:9 ("the ten-stringed"); the
  morphology a noun at every seat, the number its content. (26) עשור = 10 (UNITS gains the plene form; Exod 12:3's defective form read 10

  eating_drinking_washing_anointing_sandals_relations (the affliction's content at Lev 23:27's seat). THE IMPORT CLOSURE: naso imports
  vayikra5 and priesthood (no vows module exists); musafim imports moadim; no cycle.
- THE ENGINE'S LITERALS: DAEMON_ORDER 56 (the last law_musafim); RUN (1245, 60, 52, 0, 12, 1469, 27, 302, the four pairs, 114);
  PREVIOUS_RUN 8b's; NEWEST_RUNNER musafim; CENSUS (2171, 1254, 1245, 901, 6, 10, 9, 0, 71, 157, 125, 15, 17, 790, 265); PLACEMENT
  markers 102 / 40, events 106 / 1099 / 40; SLOTS unmoved; I5 56; the registry 327 rows; the daemons' installed_by: boot 16,
  called_from_the_tent 17, covenant_blood_thrown 13, erected 3, statute_declared 2, sentence_declared 2, milluim_blood_sprinkled 1,
  entered_the_land 1, pending 1; the installing acts: judges_appointed, covenant_blood_thrown, erected, called_from_the_tent,
  milluim_blood_sprinkled, sentence_declared, statute_declared, command_relayed (Num 36:5-9 — the tent's OUTPUT relayed in Moses' mouth,
  case_born), entered_the_land; spans 51, edges 409, pointers 166; NO pointer at Num 30 on file; run_cold_all.py names no runner (it
  globs cold_run_*.py — the 52nd joins the sweep by its name).
- THE REGISTER GATE'S CENSUS: 58 receipts — Num 30:1 NOT AMONG THEM. The finder reads כַּאֲשֶׁר צִוָּה יְהוָה ("AS the LORD commanded" — the
  lemma k/834 + 6680 + 3068); 30:1 reads כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר צִוָּה יְהוָה אֶת מֹשֶׁה ("according to ALL that the LORD commanded Moses" — k/3605 + 834 +
  6680 + 3068): THE RECEIPT FORMULA'S SECOND FORM, ELEVEN seats in the Torah (Gen 7:5 Noah; Exod 39:32, 39:42, 40:16 the sanctuary's
  three; Num 1:54, 2:34 the census's two; 8:20 the Levites; 9:5 the Passover; 30:1; Deut 1:3, 1:41), NONE in the census — the reading's
  "class of one" measured: 30:1 is the one seat of the eleven whose first word is a SPEECH verb (ויאמר "and he said"; the other ten open
  on "did", "finished", "it was", "you answered"). THE FOOTER 30:17: DAEMONS — the block (Lev 27:34, Num 30:17] holds twelve daemons
  (law_census … law_musafim); law_vows at 30:2 joins as the thirteenth, the class unmoved.
- THE DOCKET'S SCAN: 1,047 rows = 90 LINK rows in 16 works (Nedarim 51, Shevuot 9, Nazir 7, Ketubot 4, Mishnah Nedarim 4, Bava Batra 2,
  Gittin 2, Kiddushin 2, Niddah 2, Bava Metzia, Berakhot, Chagigah, Mishnah Nazir, Yevamot, Zevachim, the Rambam's introduction one each)
  + 957 TOPIC rows by address (the union rule: Mishnah Nedarim chapters 9, 10, 11 WHOLE — 10 + 8 + 12 mishnayot, 28 beyond the links;
  the folio ranges of the reading ledger's TESTING SHELF line — Nedarim 66a-91b 379 segments (the tenth and eleventh chapters to their
  end; the debt box's 87b extended to the chapter's close 91b — the wife's vows run to it), Nedarim 64a-65b 24 (the ninth chapter's
  opening — the sage's release), Nedarim 10a-13b 84 (the vow's support), Shevuot 20a-27a 271 (utterance = oath; vows against oaths;
  binding the permitted), Niddah 45b-46a 45 (the ages), Kiddushin 41b-42a 26 (the messenger), Rosh Hashanah 4a-6a 75 (the delay ban),
  Chagigah 10a 17, Sanhedrin 100a 18, Yoma 76a 15, Yevamot 87a 16, Shabbat 157a 13); 82 addresses CREDITED (the musafim docket 27, the
  Exodus triage 17, the Decalogue block 9, the Naso docket 8, the priesthood docket 7, the holiness docket 6 …). THE SECOND-LARGEST
  DOCKET OF THE WALK (9b 1,280): every row one verdict, terse for the non-material (speed ruling (c)), the credited a quick look; the
  verdict parts written to disk one per chunk (9b's form) so a compaction inside the docket loses nothing; the state doc's checkpoint
  written before the docket opens.

THE NAME: cold_run_vows.py (the 52nd runner), law_vows (the 57th daemon) — the chapter's own word (נְדָרִים "vows"; the tractate's name);
no runner or helper carries the stem (the collision guard's census: none).

THE FUNCTION — THE VOW AS A LEDGER ENTRY WITH ITS STATE MACHINE, seven cells answering the docket's rows on the ink's own nine case rows:
- F1 the_man (30:2-3): the frame (Moses' voice, no divine frame — "this is the thing which the LORD commanded" at eight Bible seats; the
  Sifrei 153:2's LIMITER: the husband annuls and does not permit, the sage permits and does not annul — two a-fortiori refused by the
  words; the sage's office the shelf's, never the ink's), the heads of the tribes (153:1 — the release of vows "by experts alone", the
  Sifrei's own addition on a FREED phrase: a DATA row sage_release, never code), "a man" (the minor excluded; the age by the identity with
  the nazirite's 6:2 — I2, NS by CALL; the ages a DATA row vow_ages: the boy thirteen and a day, the girl twelve and a day, the year before
  examined — Mishnah Niddah 5:6, Niddah 45b), the vow against the oath (the vow leans on a vowed object, "like an offering" — Nedarim
  10a-13b; the oath binds the person; 2 Kings 2:2's "as the LORD lives and as your soul lives" the Sifrei's form), "to bind a bond on his
  soul" (bind the permitted, not permit the forbidden — 153:4, Shevuot 27a; the inward acceptance — Shevuot 26b), "he shall not profane
  his word" (the sage releases others, not himself — Nedarim 81b; the two transgressions: profane, and DELAY — Deuteronomy 23:22 by the
  musafim runner's vow_deadline row READ by CALL; Deuteronomy 23 not compiled: the edge OWED or PARAMETER at the census), "according to
  all that proceeds from his mouth he shall do" (R. Akiva's equation of the utterance with the oath — 153:4); THE EFFECT: vow_bound — a
  DEBIT on the vower toward HEAVEN, its value the vow's kind and content; the vow of a man has no annuller (no authority row).
- F2 the_daughter (30:4-6): "and a woman" likened to the man (153:4), "in her youth" (twelve and a day to maturity — the DATA row; the
  mature daughter outside the father's power), "in her father's house" (his DOMAIN: the widow of betrothal in, the widow of marriage
  out — 153:4), the hearing (the deaf excluded; BY REPORT counts — "on the day of his hearing", 153:5), the silence intending HER
  (153:5 — "I thought it was my wife's": he may still annul), "then all her vows shall stand" — CONFIRMED FOR ONE HOUR, NEVER ANNULLED
  (153:5), restraint = annulment (defined by the pair at 30:9 — 153:6, 153:9), the father's hearing-day by 30:17's likening (the
  induction and the a-fortiori refused — 153:6: the hekkesh-class move (the likening by juxtaposition)), "and the LORD will forgive her"
  (the woman who broke a vow annulled without her knowing — 153:6; the a-fortiori of the swine and the lamb), "because her father
  restrained her" (his ACT — not her assurance, not a caretaker's: R. Yoshiyah; R. Yonatan: a man's messenger is as himself — the DATA row
  annul_by_messenger, the dispute fork).
- F3 the_betrothed (30:7-9): "and if she be to a man" — the betrothed (R. Yoshiyah; R. Yonatan's domain split — 153:7), THE TWO
  AUTHORITIES TOGETHER (the father and the husband annul jointly; one alone annuls nothing — Mishnah Nedarim 10:1; 156:3's R. Yishmael),
  "her vows upon her" (the vows brought from the father's house; those made in the husband's domain a-fortiori — 153:7), only the
  unconfirmed (the three-step engine ending on 30:17 — 153:7), "the utterance of her lips" = an oath (Leviticus 5:4's one seat by CALL,
  V5 — I2, 153:7), the husband's hearing and silence (153:8), the restraint (153:9), "which is upon her" — not the vows she will make
  (153:10; R. Eliezer's a-fortiori for annulling in advance against the sages — the DATA row annul_in_advance), "what comes to
  confirmation comes to annulment" (153:10 — the parallel reach of the two verbs at 30:14).
- F4 the_widow (30:10): the widow or divorcee FROM MARRIAGE — no authority (154:1: the analogy from the mature daughter, both wholly out
  of the father's domain — the common-ground move, labeled), R. Akiva's "an orphan in her father's lifetime" (Mishnah Nedarim 11:10),
  remarried she is not "a widow" (30:11 "in her husband's house" in any event — even a forbidden marriage: the high priest's widow, the
  common priest's divorcee — 154:1; PR.family's forbidden-wives table the reference), THE PRIEST'S DAUGHTER'S PAIR OF WORDS ("a widow or
  a divorced woman ... as in her youth", Leviticus 22:13 — PR.holy_food('return') by CALL, reference: the shared tokens); the vow STANDS —
  vow_bound with no annuller.
- F5 the_wife (30:11-13): "in her husband's house" — the married (30:7 having taken the betrothed — 154:1's R. Yishmael), the husband's
  hearing and the TWO SILENCES (30:12's "was silent to her" confirms; 30:15's doubled "silent, silent from day to day" is the silence to
  vex — 154:2, 156:1: the doubled verb a second content), "leave to annul all the day" (154:2's Hebrew close), "all that proceeds from her
  lips shall not stand" — the caretaker excluded (154:3's lemma), "her husband has annulled them" — his act (154:3), the forgiveness.
- F6 the_affliction_oath (30:14-16): THE FILTER on the husband's power — vows of self-affliction and vows between him and her (30:14 +
  30:17 — 155:1; R. Yoshiyah's common feature against R. Yonatan's case list: the fruits of the world / of this province / of this
  shopkeeper — Mishnah Nedarim 11:1-2; the DATA row affliction_scope), THE FATHER'S REACH ("I reasoned and reversed" — 155:1; the DATA
  row fathers_scope with the Sifrei's likening as its value and the docket's other arm if one stands), THE PARTIAL ANNULMENT (R. Yishmael:
  confirmation whole from a part, annulment whole only; R. Akiva: "confirm IT ... annul IT" — a part annuls the whole — 155:1; the DATA
  row partial_annulment; the sage's partial release releases all — Mishnah Nedarim 9:6; one vow against two), "from day to day" — THE
  DEADLINE'S TWO SETTINGS (to nightfall — the verse's own close "on the DAY of his hearing"; twenty-four hours — R. Shimon ben Yochai,
  156:1; Mishnah Nedarim 10:8) as a calendar_parameters ROW vow_annulment_window (value to_nightfall; channel received; the twenty-four-hour
  arm recorded, its day-grain rendering named — sub-day is out), the annulment on the Sabbath (Nedarim 76b-77a, Shabbat 157a — a DATA row
  annul_on_sabbath), "after his hearing" = after his CONFIRMING (156:2 — the phrase freed by 30:15's neighbor), "he shall bear her
  iniquity" (the husband enters in her place — 156:2; the phrase's first seat the suspected wife's 5:31 by CALL NS.sotah('husband_clean'),
  reference: the shared words), the a-fortiori of the two measures (the measure of good five hundred times the measure of punishment —
  Sanhedrin 100a, Yoma 76a: a DATA row measure_of_good_ratio).
- F7 the_statutes (30:17): the footer's likening BOTH WAYS in every rule stated (156:3 — the chapter's engine: four rows end on it), "in
  her youth, her father's house" (the father bounded to her youth in his house; the husband's power reaching past it — 156:3), R.
  Yishmael's betrothed maiden (156:3), the register gate (the footer DAEMONS with law_vows the thirteenth; 30:1's receipt in the census by
  the finder's SECOND FORM — its class declared from the print), the case structure computed (two "when" at the first word, seven "and if"),
  the clock words computed ("on the day of his hearing" four, "from day to day" one, "after his hearing" one), the five doubled verbs, the
  chapter's one masculine soul-token against ten "her soul".

THE STATE MACHINE (the vow on the ledger — the ink's own verbs as the ops; run on the bench by THREE case-form event kinds, every value
read from the cells): (1) vow_uttered (the vower; kind vow / oath; the vower's status — a man, a daughter in her youth in her father's
house, a betrothed maiden, a widow or divorcee from marriage, a married woman, a mature daughter; the content class — affliction /
between him and her / other; the age) → vow_bound (DEBIT, HEAVEN the counterparty) — or exempt for the minor below the examination age
(the DATA row), the examined year's vow bound on "knows to Whom she vowed" (Mishnah Niddah 5:6); (2) vow_heard (the authority — father /
husband / both; by report or not; silent, intending her) → a TIMER vow_confirmed on the vower, due = the hearing day + 1 (the evening
boundary — the day_boundary row's own convention for "until the evening": the annulment window to nightfall), no write at the hearing;
the deaf hear nothing (exempt); (3) vow_restrained (the authority; on the day or after; partial or whole; by the man or a messenger) —
on the day, by the RIGHT authority (the father over the daughter in her youth in his house; father AND husband together over the
betrothed; the husband alone over the married, for affliction vows and vows between him and her), whole (or partial by R. Akiva's arm):
world.cancel_timers(vower, 'vow_confirmed', ...) + world.close(vower, 'vow_bound', 'Num 30:6 / 30:9 / 30:13 — restrained') + vow_annulled
(HEAVEN — "and the LORD will forgive her"); after the timer's fire (the day passed — "from day to day", 30:15) → the vow stands and the
annulling husband bears her iniquity: iniquity_borne (HEAVEN on the husband, 30:16), the debit untouched; the WRONG authority (the
father over the married; the husband over a vow neither of affliction nor between them; one of the two over the betrothed) → nothing
written (the vow stands: the law's own silence, the debit open); the widow's and the man's vows have no annuller. THE ENGINE'S OWN
INTERFACE carries it whole — a timer with a due, cancel_timers, close by value, the heaven op — nothing added to world_engine.

THE DATA (parameter rows, the tradition's own vocabulary, with their arms): vow_ages (thirteen and a day / twelve and a day / the year
before examined — Mishnah Niddah 5:6; Niddah 45b-46a), sage_release (by experts alone — Sifrei 153:1; "flies in the air", Chagigah 10a;
"openings" — Mishnah Nedarim 9), annul_by_messenger (R. Yoshiyah no / R. Yonatan yes — Sifrei 153:6; Kiddushin 41b), annul_in_advance
(R. Eliezer yes / the sages no — Sifrei 153:10; Nedarim 75a), partial_annulment (R. Yishmael whole only / R. Akiva a part annuls the
whole — Sifrei 155:1), affliction_scope (R. Yoshiyah's feature / R. Yonatan's cases — Sifrei 155:1; Mishnah Nedarim 11:1-2), fathers_scope
(likened to the husband — Sifrei 155:1; the other arm from the docket if one stands), betrothed_authority (joint — Mishnah Nedarim
10:1; R. Yishmael 156:3), annul_on_sabbath (Nedarim 76b-77a; Shabbat 157a), measure_of_good_ratio (five hundred — Sanhedrin 100a; Yoma
76a), the annulment window a calendar_parameters row (vow_annulment_window — the registry, read by the runner as data), the vow_deadline
row the musafim runner's, READ by CALL — never re-declared.

THE DAEMON law_vows (given_at Num 30:2; installed_by BOOT — THE FORM FOR A LAW IN MOSES' VOICE, decided here and typed at the types
step: the installing acts are institution-erecting events keyed to a `kind: institution` entity, and Moses' relay to the heads of the
tribes erects none — 36:6's relayed command is the tent's OUTPUT on a case (command_relayed, case_born), this chapter has no case; so
the walk's standing setting, boot, with the class NAMED in the registry's comment ("a statute relayed in Moses' voice with no divine
frame — 'this is the thing which the LORD commanded', 30:2; the book's other such chapter 36; the second pass (D2) decides whether the
relay needs the tent standing"), and a COMPILE_DEBT line for the second pass): on vows_law_spoken (Num 30:2-17 — ONE line, form speech,
subject moses, the field `to` the heads of the tribes as DATA, the field `heads` the nine case rows) → commanded on israel_people (value
"the statutes of vows — Num 30:2-17", the one write; no timer, no close, no entity); the three state-machine kinds and the seven exam
kinds (mans_vow_case, daughters_vow_case, betrothed_vow_case, widows_vow_case, wifes_vow_case, affliction_oath_case, statutes_case)
dispatch to the cells with LITERAL effects (2b's form).

THE TAPE: ONE LINE vows_law_spoken page_order at (40, 6, 1), sorted by the stitcher between the calendar's line (28:1-29:39) and the
tribes' plea (36:1-4); NO marker (no date in the chapter); no entity added; one write (commanded on israel_people, its tenth entry).

THE CHECKPOINTS (CV1-CV9): CV1 THE LINE — on the tape at (40, 6, 1), page_order, after the calendar's line and before the 36:1-4 plea,
one event; CV2 THE WRITE — commanded on israel_people 9 → 10, no timer set (60), no close (114); CV3 THE PARSER — no cardinal, no ordinal
in 30:1-17, the three starred oath-tokens; CV4 THE CASE STRUCTURE — "when" at the first word of 30:3 and 30:4, "and if" at 30:6, 7, 9,
11, 13, 15, 16 (computed on the tokens); CV5 THE CLOCK WORDS — "on the day of his hearing" ×4 (30:6, 8, 13, 15), "from day to day"
(30:15), "after his hearing" (30:16); CV6 THE STATE MACHINE on the bench — the confirmation by the timer's fire at day + 1, the annulment
with the timer cancelled and the debit closed, the iniquity borne after confirmation, the wrong authority writing nothing (the scene's
tuple predicted by script before the runner runs); CV7 THE CALLEES — the nazirite's form, 5:31's clean / not clear, Lev 5:4's option
template, 22:13's return, the vow_deadline row's value; CV8 THE REGISTER GATE — the footer 30:17 DAEMONS with thirteen daemons; the
receipt census 58 → 69 by the second form, Num 30:1 present, its class and the ten other seats' classes declared from the print; CV9 THE
REST — 9b's RUN exactly with the line dropped.

THE PREDICTION'S ARITHMETIC (the RUN tuple typed at the literals step, after the stitcher's census): events +1 (the one line); timers
set unchanged (60 — the bench's timers are the scene's, off the tape), fired 52; cancels 0; retro-writes 12; writes +1 (commanded);
daemons fired +1 (law_vows returns one effect); entities +0 (302); the four pairs unchanged; closes 114. RUN = (1246, 60, 52, 0, 12,
1470, 28, 302, the four pairs, 114). THE REST: NEWEST_RUNNER = 'vows', PREVIOUS_RUN = 9b's RUN EXACTLY (no marker in the span; the one
line dropped). PLACEMENT: events page_order 1099 → 1100, the rest unmoved; markers unmoved. CENSUS: on tape 1245 → 1246, history 1254 →
1255, kinds 790 → 791, markers 157 and subjects 265 unmoved (moses stands as a subject), closes 71 unchanged; scanned / case by the
stitcher's print. SLOTS: read at the print (30:2 carries no day-word).

THE PROBES: census_probes.py gains R54-R56 (Num 30:3, 30:11, 30:14 = [] — the starred oath-tokens; the parser unchanged, the regressions
green before and after: 171 → 174); register_probes.py's R2 RETYPED TO FAIL FIRST — the receipt census 69 with Num 30:1 present

THE TYPES (by script, add_types_musafim.py's form): the tape kind vows_law_spoken (speech; witness 30:2-17; the corpus strings
num_30_vows STEP_Nm_30_1 … STEP_Nm_30_17, claims MT30A-01 … -07); the three state-machine kinds (vow_uttered, vow_heard, vow_restrained —
form case, witnesses 30:3, 30:5, 30:6); the seven exam kinds; the FOUR effects (vow_bound debit, vow_confirmed status, vow_annulled
heaven, iniquity_borne heaven — each `he` the verse's words from the DB, the ink verbs machine-verified); no registry row; law_vows' block
(given_at Num 30:2, installed_by boot with the class named, the watches); the functions block (vows: the_man … the_statutes WRAPPED);
the span [[Num, 30, 1, 17]]; THE EDGES — vows → naso CALL (reference: 6:2's vow-identity and 5:31's "bear her iniquity" — the shared
tokens), → vayikra5 CALL (reference: Leviticus 5:4's utterance-root — the Sifrei's identity), → priesthood CALL (reference: Leviticus
22:13's "a widow or a divorced woman", "in her youth" — the shared tokens), → musafim CALL (reference: 29:39 "besides your vows" —
the vow_deadline row read), → moadim CALL (reference: the affliction-root of Leviticus 23:27 at 30:14), and whatever else the token
census demands (Deuteronomy 23:22-24 not compiled — OWED or PARAMETER; 32:24 forward); the calendar_parameters row vow_annulment_window;
sequence → vows the registration edge; I5 56 → 57.

THE ORDER (1b's, as at 9b): the design (this) → the probes (census R54-R56 green; register R2 to FAIL) → the receipt finder's second
form → register_probes 6/6 → THE STATE DOC'S CHECKPOINT (the docket may cross a compaction) → the docket (1,047 rows; the parts on disk)
→ the types by script → the gates to FAIL → the runner (the guard's count typed after the first graded run; the scene's tuple predicted
by script) → the recorder → the stitcher (no marker row; the census typed from its print) → the literals and CV1-CV9 → the tape run (the
tuple predicted first) → the probe gates (census 174, installation 57, clock, sequence, cursor, view, journal, population, register) →
THE REGISTER GATE --strict (the eleven seats declared from the print; a paid seat's key AND body deleted) → the sweep (52 runners) →
the records (this file's as-built, COMPILE_DEBT's sitting-10 box PAID with the second pass's line, MOVE_CATALOG if a move is new,
RESEARCH_LOG, the state doc, THE_STEPS, THE_BRIEFING, RESUME, memory).

## Sitting 10b — AS BUILT (2026-09-12; the compile of the vows, run across compaction point #143 (before the docket) and one more compaction inside the
literals step; every step of THE ORDER above run in its order)

THE MEASUREMENTS (scratchpad vows_compile_measure.py + .out, vows_docket_scan.py + .out): the parser clean at 30:1-17 and 31:1 with the three oath-tokens
STARRED; the tape at (40, 6, 1), 302 entities, 157 markers, 1245 events, the eight musaf_owed timers PENDING, no event citing Num 30; the callees live

commanded") has ELEVEN Torah seats (Gen 7:5; Exod 39:32, 39:42, 40:16; Num 1:54, 2:34, 8:20, 9:5, 30:1; Deut 1:3, 1:41), 30:1 the one opening on a speech verb.

THE PROBES: census_probes.py R54-R56 (Num 30:3, 30:11, 30:14 = []) — 174/174 on the unchanged parser (no rule, no diff); register_probes.py R2 RETYPED TO
FAIL (69 receipts, 'Num 30:1' present) → 5/6 → register_census.receipts() gained the second form (k/3605 + 834 + 6680 + 3068 within four) → R6's tally
FAILED on the print ({'CHAPTER': 7, 'NONE': 25, 'ACT': 21, 'EVENT': 5, 'CLOSE': 11}; CLOSE 9 → 11) → R6 retyped from the print → 6/6.

THE DOCKET (logic/oral_triage/num_30_vows_exam_2026-09-12.md, written by scratchpad write_vows_docket.py from THIRTEEN verdict parts A-M on disk, one per
~240-line chunk of the 3,143-line dump, so the compaction point #143 written before it lost nothing): 1,047 rows = 90 link rows in 16 works + 957 topic
rows (Mishnah Nedarim 9-11 whole; Nedarim 66a-91b, 64a-65b, 10a-13b; Shevuot 20a-27a; Niddah 45b-46a; Kiddushin 41b-42a; Rosh Hashanah 4a-6a; Chagigah
10a; Sanhedrin 100a; Yoma 76a; Yevamot 87a; Shabbat 157a); LAW 241 / DERIVATION 384 / DISPUTE 85 / CONTEXT 334 / OUTSIDE 3 (the export's three EMPTY
segments — Nedarim 66b:9, 79a:10, 13b:6 — a verdict class for a segment with no text); 82 credited; 15 crowns; the cite index; coverage COMPUTED; lint 0
after ONE regeneration inside the writing step (a hyphenated compound in part I rewritten in words).

THE TYPES (scratchpad add_types_vows.py): eleven kinds (vows_law_spoken speech; vow_uttered / vow_heard / vow_restrained the state machine's case kinds;
seven exam kinds — registry 1044), FOUR effects (vow_bound debit, vow_confirmed status, vow_annulled heaven, iniquity_borne heaven; each `he` the verse's
words from the DB — registry 1001), law_vows (given_at Num 30:2, installed_by BOOT with the class NAMED in the comment: a law relayed in Moses' voice with
no divine frame erects no institution — 30:2 and 36:6 the class; the second pass's question filed — 57 daemons), the functions block vows (the_man …

on the first typed pass and one on the second, every one a slice index, a token form or a seat outside the Torah: print every leg, retype from the print,
scope to the Torah when the claim is the Torah's. A HYPHENATED ENGLISH COMPOUND IS FLAGGED BY THE LINT — reword and regenerate inside the writing step. THE
HEADS ARE CHECKED AGAINST THE ROWS' OWN CITATIONS BY SCRIPT — an assert (ROW_CITES), not a reading. THE TWO FILES ARE READ AGAINST EACH OTHER AT EVERY ROW
— the English reversed an arm, inserted an ancestor, supplied the Talmud and the Mishnah, dropped a rule about rules and three attributions; the Hebrew
misquoted a lemma and ran an identity's leg backward: the Hebrew row is the shelf, the English a witness with its own defects. THE SHELF'S SILENCE IS
COMPUTED (165 verses). THE FRACTION CLASS MEASURED CORPUS-WIDE BEFORE THE COMPILE (six seats; the class named and left at R29 holds until the compile teaches
it). THE ARITHMETIC IS THE INK'S OWN CHECKSUM, AND THE UNWRITTEN NUMBER IS COMPUTED (the Levites' 8,400). THE STORE'S GLOSSES ARE STRONG'S HOMONYMS — the
override file, never the frozen unit. THE READING SITTING'S SHAPE HELD IN A NEW THREAD FROM THE COPIED FORMS: dump → measure → asserts (14 → 1 → 0) → rows →
writer (0 misses; lint 1 → 0 inside the step) → manifest (11/11) → seat → ritual (13 PASS) → the fold predicted and matched.

  the ten" and the nine parts read); and the class's POETIC form found beside them — Job 9:3 and 33:23 אחת/אחד מני אלף ("one from a thousand"
  with the poetic מני "from") read [1, 1000] since the parser's first day: EIGHT seats. The parser's own instrument: the article-bearing numeral
  alone is a name or a noun (1b's rule — החמשים "the fifty" refused), the מ-prefixed unit (מחמש "from five") is no numeral to _bare (the
  from-prefix is not in its prefix list), and מן "from" (lemma 4480) closes the phrase. THE READ FORM DECIDED HERE: the ratio REPLACES the one —
  "one of the N" = Fraction(1, N) (the walk's own fraction class at 4b reads "a quarter of the hin" as Fraction(1, 4) with the % mark; the
  tradition names the rate as a fraction: "one fiftieth", Mishnah Terumot 4:3) — not a pair [1, N]: the N is the denominator of a rate, never
  a count (31:28's "five hundred" is no five hundred souls; the register gate's unit rule confirms it: a rate is a MEASURE, not a checksum).

THE NAME: cold_run_midian.py (the 53rd runner), law_midian (the 58th daemon) — the chapter's own word (מדין "Midian"; the war's name in the
tradition, "the war of Midian", Yevamot 61a:5); no runner or helper carries the stem (the collision guard's census: none).

THE FUNCTION — THE WAR AS A RUN OF THREE OPEN DEBITS, THE DIVISION AS A CHECKED TABLE, seven cells answering the docket's rows on the ink's own
paragraphs:
- F1 the_vengeance (31:1-6): the command "avenge the vengeance of the children of Israel from the Midianites" (the article on the Midianites
  at 25:17 and 31:2 alone — THE COMMAND AND ITS RUN SHARE THE WORD; Moses' relay turns it into "the vengeance of the LORD", 31:3 — Sifrei
  157:2); "afterward you shall be gathered to your people" (the SEQUENCE constraint — Moses' death after the war; the checkpoint waits at
  Deuteronomy 34 beside barred_from_the_land's close); the muster "a thousand to a tribe, a thousand to a tribe" (31:4 — the distributive pair;
  31:5's 12,000 = 1,000 × 12 THE INK'S OWN PRODUCT, a CHECK cell; R. Yishmael's 24,000 — the doubling read twice — a DATA row muster_count
  with R. Akiva's 12,000 the ink's; Levi in or out a DATA row levi_in_the_muster — the Hebrew's "to include", the English's "to exclude",
  Sifrei 157:3 both arms recorded); "were delivered" (31:5 וימסרו — the deliver-root's two Torah seats both here; the three readings of Sifrei
  157:4 a DATA row delivered_how); Phinehas sent "with the holy vessels and the trumpets of the alarm in his hand" (31:6 — the anointed for war,
  Sotah 43a; the Urim, the ark, the frontplate: DATA row holy_vessels); THE TRUMPETS' DEBIT: moses' commanded the_trumpets (10:2 "make for
  yourself two trumpets") CLOSED BY VALUE at 31:6 — the trumpets exist in the priest's hand, the making implied by the run and never narrated
  (the class named in the note: A RUN BY CARRYING); 10:9's war clause ("when you go to war in your land against the oppressor... you shall
  sound") is the REFERENCE read by CALL BH.trumpets('oppression', 'war') — NO SOUNDING NARRATED: nothing written for the alarm (the law's own
  silence; 2 Chronicles 13:12 the phrase's other seat, Abijah's war, where the priests DO sound); the effect counted 12,000 on the-men-of-war.
- F2 the_war (31:7-12): "and they warred against Midian as the LORD commanded Moses" (31:7 — THE RECEIPT that closes israel_people's
  harass_the_midianites BY VALUE (the 7b lesson) and moses' avenge_the_midianites; the register gate's 31:7 turns CLOSE); "they killed every
  male" (Shechem's phrase, Genesis 34:25 — its only other seat); the five kings by name (31:8 [5]; Joshua 13:21 retells them as "the princes of
  Sihon, dwelling in the land" — three deltas, a RUN_CITATION; Zur = Cozbi's father by BK.DATA['cozbi_and_zur'] READ; the party
  the-kings-of-midian one entity, the five names its value — the registry's the_five_kings is Genesis 14's and chur Exodus 17's); "and Balaam
  son of Beor they killed with the sword" (31:8 — slain on balaam, cp israel, the value from BK.DATA['balaam_death'] READ by CALL with its two
  settings; the ass's sword clause 22:29 CLOSED IN THE READING'S SENSE — a crown noted in the entry, no entry of its own); the captives (31:9 —
  taken_captive on the-captives-of-midian: the women and the little ones, cp israel), the spoil (31:9 — spoil_taken on israel_people, cp
  the-midianites: their cattle, their flocks, all their goods), the cities and castles burned (31:10 — burned_in_fire on the-midianites: their
  cities in their dwellings and all their castles; Onkelos' "houses of worship" the DATA row castles_reading); the bringing to Moses at the plains
  of Moab (31:12 — the line's field).
- F3 the_sentence (31:13-18): Moses' wrath (31:14 ויקצף "and he was wroth" — the wrath-verb with Moses as subject at three Torah seats, Exodus
  16:20, Leviticus 10:16, here; Sifrei 157:9 "anger begets error" at three places, "in the name of its sayer" — mark_of_anger on moses, a MOVE:
  the error's evidence the statute in Eleazar's mouth at 31:21, Pesachim 66b); "have you let every female live?" (31:15 — the midwives' verb);

verse not printed is printed before it is sliced. THE SHELF'S SILENCE IS PROVED BY POSITION AND ITS CROSS-CITING ROWS BY A WHOLE-EXPORT SCAN — three rows
of other chapters cite 32, credited with a quick look, never counted as rows on the chapter. THE STORE'S PAIR — a written-and-read pair shows as one token

  the Torah (the release is Joshua's); the exam's scene writes it on Mishnah Shekalim 3:2's clerk. THE DATA rows: doubled_condition (Mishnah

THE INK, COMPUTED (the measurement passes FIRST, the asserts typed from the print):
- THE PARSER MEASURED FIRST (the standing rule): FIVE number verses in fifty-six, every one read — 33:3 "on the fifteenth day of the first month" [15]
  with the ordinals [1, 1]; 33:8 "a way of three days" [3]; 33:9 "twelve springs of water and seventy palm trees" [12, 70] (Exodus 15:27 the same);
  33:38 THE DATE — the year and the month as ORDINALS [40, 5] (the article-bearing numeral, rule 20 — the Torah's one seat of the form) and the day

  its two ordinals and its number into day_in and CM2 checks (40, 5, 1): the reading's measurement equals the tape's marker. THE INK'S OWN CHECKSUM by
  the same parser: Aaron 83 at the exodus (Exodus 7:7 [80, 83]) + 40 = 123; Moses 80 + 40 = 120 (Deuteronomy 34:7); the brothers three years apart at
  both ends. THE TWO DATE FORMS: Deuteronomy 1:3's fortieth-year date is cardinal ([40, 11, 1] by the number reader) — a date checkpoint at Deuteronomy
  takes both readers.
- THE FRAMES AND THE REGISTER: NINETY-THREE narrative verbs in forty-seven verses — eighty-four the pair "journeyed" / "camped" (FORTY-TWO each) and
  nine others (Moses wrote; the camp turned back — singular, Exodus 14:2's jussive (the wish form, "let them turn back") fulfilled; passed and went; came; Aaron went up and died; the
  Canaanite heard; the LORD spoke); ONE divine frame (33:50) after forty-nine verses with none and ONE speech (33:51-56); the chapters of Numbers
  without a frame unchanged (22-24, 29, 30, 32, 36). "Journeyed" in forty-two verses (33:3, 33:5-48), "camped" in forty-two (33:5-49); forty-one with
  both, 33:3 the journey alone (the date line), 33:49 the camp alone (the last camp's extent); Onkelos keeps both counts.
- THE FORTY-TWO ON THE INK'S OWN COUNT: the departure from Rameses told TWICE (33:3 with its date, 33:5 as the itinerary's first line) and the camp in
  the plains of Moab TWICE (33:48 the arrival, 33:49 its extent "from Beth-jeshimoth to Abel-shittim") — the PLACES named are forty-two: Rameses and
  forty-one camps (thirty-three at 33:5-37, eight at 33:41-48); the departures' after-tokens forty distinct; 144 name-tokens, 108 distinct; Moseroth
  seven camps before Mount Hor by index.
- THE HEADING AND THE FRAME: "these are the journeys of the children of Israel" — 10:28 and 33:1, the same first four words; sixty-three verse-initial
  "these" headings in the Torah; "by their hosts" sixteen seats, every one in Numbers; "by the hand of Moses and Aaron" — 33:1 and Psalm 77:21, the
  phrase's two Bible seats; "AND MOSES WROTE" four Torah seats — the covenant's words, THE JOURNEYS, this Torah, this song (the tablets at Exodus 34:28
  and Deuteronomy 10:4 the verb's other two); 33:2's chiasm ("their goings out" two Bible seats, "their journeys" six — the cloud's stages); "by the mouth
  of the LORD" twenty-one Bible seats, eighteen Torah, fifteen in Numbers — Moses WROTE by it and Aaron WENT UP by it; 33:7's "mouth" Pi-hahiroth's, the
  homograph. ONKELOS "by the WORD of the LORD" at both.
- THE DATE LINE: "on the fifteenth day of the first month" the full phrase one seat; Leviticus 23:6 and 28:17 date the feast of unleavened bread "on
  the fifteenth day of THIS month" — the departure's date is the feast's; Exodus 12 gives the fourteenth at evening and "that selfsame day" — 33:3 alone
  writes the fifteenth of the going out; "THE MORROW OF THE PASSOVER" two Bible seats — 33:3 (out of Egypt) and Joshua 5:11 (the land's bread, the manna
  ceasing on the morrow): the run's two ends on one date-word; "WITH A HIGH HAND" three Torah seats (Exodus 14:8 the same going out; 15:30 — the Sifrei
  112:2's verse; 33:3), ONKELOS "with bared head" at 15:30 and 33:3 alone and the Sifrei's own gloss on 15:30 "one who bares his face" the same root;
  33:4 "Egypt was burying" (the participle's two Bible seats), "AND ON THEIR GODS THE LORD EXECUTED JUDGMENTS" one seat — THE RUN of Exodus 12:12's "I
  will execute judgments" (the future at Exodus 12:12 and Ezekiel 25:11 alone, the perfect nowhere else): Exodus narrates the firstborn and never the
  judgments on the gods — the itinerary alone records that run, forty years on. ONKELOS "on their idols" (25:2's word).
- THE STATIONS AGAINST THEIR FIRST TELLINGS, ON THE TOKENS: 33:5 opens as Exodus 12:37; 33:6 IS Exodus 13:20 with one word added; 33:7 fulfils Exodus
  14:2's jussive; 33:8 "passed through the midst of the sea" (Nehemiah 9:11 the other seat), "a way of three days in the wilderness of ETHAM" for
  Exodus 15:22's SHUR (each name one seat; "a way of three days" six Torah seats — Moses' request of Pharaoh walked after the sea); 33:9 Elim's twelve
  springs and seventy palms word for word with Exodus 15:27; 33:10 THE CAMP BY THE RED SEA, 33:12-13 DOPHKAH AND ALUSH — three stations Exodus never
  names; 33:14 Rephidim's water clause in another order (each order one seat; the defective spelling with Exodus 17:8's); 33:15 "camped in the wilderness
  of Sinai" (Exodus 19:2's singular verb beside it); 33:16-17 chapter 11's graves and Hazeroth (ONKELOS "the graves of those who demanded" at all four
  seats of the name); 33:18 RITHMAH for 12:16's "wilderness of Paran" — the spies' base under two names; Taberah no station.
- THE SEVENTEEN STATIONS 33:19-35: ELEVEN of the chapter's stations named NOWHERE ELSE in the Bible (Dophkah, Alush, Rithmah, Rissah, Kehelathah,
  Makheloth, Mithkah, Hashmonah, Abronah, Zalmonah, Punon — computed by every form's seats); THREE names only here whose consonants are common words
  (Mount Shepher — the ram's horn of Exodus 19:16; Haradah — "trembling"; Tahath — "under", a place by the morphology); Rimmon-perez shares Naaman's
  "house of Rimmon" (2 Kings 5:18), Terah carries Abraham's father's name (eleven seats), Libnah a Judah city's (nineteen); MOSEROTH, BENE-JAAKAN,
  HOR-HAGGIDGAD and JOTBATHAH are DEUTERONOMY 10:6-7's four in another order; Ezion-geber Deuteronomy 2:8's and Solomon's port. THE DEUTERONOMY
  DIVERGENCE, OBSERVED: Deuteronomy 10:6 runs "from Beeroth-bene-jaakan to Moserah; THERE AARON DIED" — the itinerary runs Moseroth then Bene-jaakan and
  puts the death at Mount Hor seven camps on; the declared shelf silent on the two orders (the Sifrei on Deuteronomy not declared).
- KADESH, MOUNT HOR, THE DEATH AND ARAD: "that is Kadesh" the identity idiom at five Bible seats (Genesis 14:7's "En-mishpat, that is Kadesh"); "in
  the edge of the land of Edom" one seat beside 20:23's "on the border" one seat; Mount Hor twelve Torah seats; ONKELOS Rekem for Kadesh at ten seats
  of Onkelos Numbers and the Hebrew's own Rekem, the Midianite king of 31:8, the eleventh; "Hor the mountain" at 33:37-41 and 34:8. 33:38-39: "in the
  fortieth year" (the article form) two Bible seats (1 Chronicles 26:31 David's); "of the going out of the children of Israel from the land of Egypt"
  three Bible seats — Exodus 19:1, 33:38, 1 Kings 6:1: THE ERA'S THREE STAMPS in that form; "in the fifth month" the Torah's one seat; "at his death"
  two Bible seats — Aaron's and Moses'. 33:40: "and the Canaanite king of Arad heard" — 21:1 and this; the itinerary keeps the hearing and drops the war,
  the captives, the vow and Hormah, adds "in the land of Canaan"; its place right after the death — the Sifrei's 82:1 reads 21:1 so.
- THE LAST STATIONS AGAINST CHAPTER 21: Zalmonah and Punon named here alone (21:4 names no camp before Oboth); Oboth and Iye-abarim 21:10-11's; of
  chapter 21's stations after Iye-abarim (the Zered, the Arnon, Beer, Mattanah, Nahaliel, Bamoth, the valley of Pisgah) NOT ONE is in the itinerary, and
  its Dibon-gad, Almon-diblathaim and the mountains of Abarim are not in 21 — the two lists share Oboth, Iye-abarim and Moab's name alone (the
  name-tokens intersected); DIBON-GAD — Gad built Dibon (32:34), the itinerary's witness to chapter 32; Almon-diblathaim Jeremiah 48:22's
  Beth-diblathaim; "the MOUNTAINS of Abarim before Nebo" (plural) beside 27:12's "the mountain of Abarim"; "the plains of Moab" eight Torah seats, "by
  the Jordan at Jericho" seven; Beth-jeshimoth Reuben's (Joshua 13:20) and Moab's (Ezekiel 25:9), Abel-shittim one seat, Shittim Peor's, the spies' and
  the crossing's, Micah's. ONKELOS "the plains" one word at eight seats, "the plain of Shittim", "the fords of the Abarim".
- THE COMMAND (33:50-56): the frame's two seats (33:50, 35:1); "when you pass over the Jordan" 33:51, 35:10, Deuteronomy 11:31; "drive out all the
  inhabitants of the land" one seat (Exodus 23:31's promise its kin); "their FIGURED STONES" Leviticus 26:1's word; "their MOLTEN IMAGES" one seat (the
  calf's word; "from Succoth" the consonantal look-alike); "their HIGH PLACES you shall DEMOLISH" — Leviticus 26:30's curse in the same verb on the same
  object; the other iconoclasm commands (Exodus 23:24, 34:13, Deuteronomy 7:5, 12:2-3) name altars, pillars, asherim and graven images — 33:52's three
  are its own; 33:53 "dwell in it" Deuteronomy 11:31's, "to possess it" Leviticus 20:24's, ONKELOS supplying "the inhabitants" where the Hebrew has "the
  land"; 33:54 IS 26:52-56 RESTATED TO THE PEOPLE with the first verb turned plural and the second left singular — the number switching inside the verse
  (computed on the morphology; Onkelos makes both plural); "by lot" four Torah seats; "to whom the lot goes out, his it shall be" one; "by the tribes of
  your fathers" one; 33:55 THE NEGATIVE ARM — "thorns in your eyes and pricks in your sides" run back REVERSED by Joshua 23:13 ("scourges in your sides
  and pricks in your eyes"), Judges 2:3 "for sides"; "leave over" the Passover's verb (Exodus 12:10); "harass" 25:18's Midian word; ONKELOS reads the
  figure as "bands taking up arms against you and camps surrounding you"; 33:56 "as I thought" Isaiah 14:24's, ONKELOS "as I planned".
- THE STORE'S GLOSSES READ BACK: "and-grave" (wrote), "the-pretermission" (the Passover), "be-high-actively", "sentence" (judgments), "eye" (springs),
  "in-cord" (the border), "and-wander-away" (destroy), "figure" / "pouring-over" / "elevation", "desolate" (demolish), "in-pebble" (by lot), "jut-over"
  (leave over), "to-brier", "and-cramp" (harass), "compare" (thought), and the "?" at Pi-, Baal-, Kibroth-, Beth- and Abel- — twenty-one rows added to
  the display layer's override file by reference; the frozen unit untouched.

⚠ LESSONS (8): THE CITATION SCANNER READS THE EXPORT'S ABBREVIATION AND ITS MARKS — "(Bamidbar 33:n)" and a straight-quote chapter mark found ZERO rows;
"(Ibid. 33:n)" and the gershayim found the one that is there (133:3): a report of zero is worth its coverage line, and the coverage line must name the
forms scanned. THE POINTED TEXT IS NOT THE PLAIN — Onkelos's renderings searched on the pointed Aramaic returned nothing at eleven seats; strip the
points, then search (the same lesson as the shelf's search, now on the translation). TEN SLICE INDICES IN FIFTEEN FALLS — the fifth instance, and the
worst: the itinerary's short verses made the indices look safe; print the verse, then slice, whatever the verse's length. A PISKA'S HEAD IS ITS FIRST
ROW'S CITATION, NOT THE ROW'S VERSE — 112 opens at 15:27 and its second row is on 15:30. THE TWO DATE FORMS HAVE TWO READERS — 33:38's article-bearing
year and month are the ordinal reader's, Deuteronomy 1:3's cardinal date the number reader's: a date checkpoint takes both. A HOMOGRAPH PLURAL IS
ANOTHER TOKEN (Psalm 73:7's "figured" is not 33:52's form); "THEIR HIGH PLACES" IS THE CONSONANTS OF "AT THEIR DEATH" — the morphology decides. THE
READING SITTING'S SHAPE HELD: dump → measure (three passes) → asserts (15 → 0) → rows → writer (0 misses; lint 0 first write) → manifest (12/12) → seat →
ritual (13 PASS) → the fold predicted and matched.

THE NAME: cold_run_journeys.py (the 55th runner), law_journeys (the 60th daemon) — the chapter's own first word after the heading's "these are"
("the journeys of the children of Israel", 33:1; the unit's num_33_journeys); no runner or helper carries the stem (the collision guard's census:
none; the erection's cloud cell has an ASK named 'journeys', a string, no def).

THE FUNCTION — THE ITINERARY AS A RECORD WRITTEN AT THE RUN'S END: the chapter is a LIST written by Moses in the fortieth year of forty-two places
the tape already walked, with two dates that are the tape's own markers, one run recorded nowhere else (the judgments on Egypt's gods), one
retold death with its date and age, one retold hearing, and ONE LAW in the divine voice whose runs lie past the Torah. THE STATIONS' SHAPE (the
design's question of COMPILE_DEBT's box (a), decided on the measurements): THE FORTY-TWO ARE A DATA ROW, NOT TAPE LINES. Reasons: (1) A RETELLING
NEVER WRITES AN ACT TWICE — sixteen camps stand on the ledger as encamped_at statuses at their first tellings' own days, ten of them under the
tape's markers; a second encamped_at at Succoth would be a second write of Exodus 12:37's act; (2) THE CHAPTER'S LINES LAND AT (40, 6, 1) — the
counter's day, the record's day; a status "encamped at Dophkah" written at year forty would put a year-one camp at the run's end, false on the
clock: the eighteen stations named nowhere else have NO day of their own in the ink (the itinerary gives none) and a date they cannot carry; (3)
THE INK'S OWN VERB FOR THE CHAPTER IS "WROTE" (33:2) — the act the chapter narrates is the WRITING, whose value is the list; so the list is the
value of ONE status written by that act, and the stations live in the DATA row the_stations (index 0-41, the Hebrew name with its gloss, the
itinerary's verse, the first telling's verse outside 33 or None, the tape's witness — a marker, an encamped_at status, or none) and in the
status's value. THE CHECKPOINT reads the tape's sixteen camps against the list (which match by the itinerary's names — Succoth, Etham, Marah,
Elim, Sin, Rephidim, Sinai, Kadesh, Mount Hor, Oboth and Iye-abarim, the plains of Moab; which do not — Goshen (before Rameses), the wilderness of
Shur (33:8 says Etham), the Red Sea way of 21:4 (a road, not a camp), Mattanah to Pisgah (21:18-20's stations absent from the itinerary — the
reading's finding), Shittim (33:49's Abel-shittim, the last camp's extent)). Five cells on the ink's own paragraphs:
- F1 the_heading_and_the_writing (33:1-2): "these are the journeys" (the sixty-three verse-initial "these" headings of the Torah computed; 10:28's
  first four words the same), "by their hosts" (sixteen seats, all in Numbers), "by the hand of Moses and Aaron" (33:1 and Psalm 77:21 the phrase's
  two Bible seats — OBSERVED), "AND MOSES WROTE their goings out by their journeys BY THE MOUTH OF THE LORD" (33:2 — the four Torah seats of "and
  Moses wrote": Exodus 24:4, this, Deuteronomy 31:9, 31:22, a DATA row the_four_writings; "by the mouth of the LORD" twenty-one Bible seats, the

rows owed their verdicts, 8b's fifth defect class); 139 credited with a quick look; the crowns; the cite index; coverage COMPUTED; lint 1 → 0 inside the
writing step (one rabbinic-genre word reworded). THE CROWNS: THE FORTIETH YEAR'S WALK IN THE SHELF'S OWN ORDER (Seder Olam Rabbah 9:2) — three months at
Kadesh, Aaron 123 at Mount Hor, Arad's hearing, THE RETREAT OF SEVEN STATIONS TO MOSERAH reconciling Deuteronomy 10:6's "there Aaron died" with 33:38,
and the itinerary's own count agrees — Moseroth stands seven camps before Mount Hor by index (measured at the reading, asserted at the compile: the
shelf's seven = the ink's seven; the chukat runner's row has this row as its source); THE ERA'S NEW YEAR FROM THE CHAPTER'S DATE (Rosh Hashanah
2b:9-3a:13 — the fortieth year in Av and in Shevat one year, so not Tishrei; the second year in Nisan and Iyar, so not Iyar; Exodus 19:1 without "the
second year", so not Sivan; 1 Kings 6:1 the kings' count from the exodus: THE ERA'S THREE STAMPS the shelf's own chain of proof, with the verbal analogy
"the fortieth year" / "the fortieth year" TAUGHT for Deuteronomy 1:3's bare date — the two date readers meeting on the shelf); THE MORROW OF THE PASSOVER
AT BOTH ENDS (Berakhot 9a:25 redeemed at evening, left by day; Kiddushin 37b:14-38a:4 the omer's arm and the manna's — forty years less thirty days from
the sixteenth of Iyar, THE TAPE'S MARKER at Exodus 16:13, to the sixteenth of Nisan, and the cakes of 33:3's fifteenth thirty days); MOSES' SEVENTH OF

## Sitting 14 — THE BORDERS, Numbers 34:1-29 (2026-09-12; the owner: "Go" after the #154 rereads, on the ruling READ THEN COMPILE): the reading and the unit

THE DRAFT: one — num_34_borders 34:1-29 (29 of 29 verses, computed): the portion Masei's second chapter whole; the next draft, num_35_refuge_cities, opens
at 35:1 (asserted). Read in ONE pass, one ledger. The forms copied from sitting 13's scripts in World/step9/forms_numbers_walk/ and edited (bor_dump.py,
bor_measure1.py, bor_measure2.py, bor_ink.py, bor_legs.py, bor_rows_onkelos_a.py / _b.py, write_bor_ledger.py, write_bor_manifest.py, seat_bor.py,
bor_chain.sh, patch_overrides_bor.py, write_bor_records.py; every one copied into the forms folder at the close).

THE SHELF, BY POSITION (bor_ink.py's asserts): the Sifrei on Numbers has NO piska on the chapter — 158 (31:22) is followed by 159 (35:9): the shelf is
SILENT from 31:25 to 35:8 (165 verses, computed at sitting 11), and this chapter is the fourth and last stretch of that silence read on the translation
alone (the shelf RETURNS at 35:9). THE "FOUND BY POSITION" CLAUSE run on the whole export in FOUR FORMS — the English's "(Bamidbar 34:n)" and "(Ibid.
34:n)", the Hebrew's chapter mark with the gershayim (the double-stroke mark), and A FOURTH FORM sitting 13's scan had not looked for: the Hebrew's own
ibid., the word "there" before the chapter mark. ONE row of another chapter cites Numbers 34 — 1:2 (on 5:2, the sending out of the unclean): Rabbi Shimon
ben Yochai's reading of "command" — everywhere it entails expense (Leviticus 24:2 the oil, 35:2 the Levites' cities, 28:2 the offerings) EXCEPT ONE, 34:2
"command the children of Israel and say to them: when you come to the land of Canaan" — "impel them to the division of the land"; the Hebrew row cites it
as "ibid. 34"; THE CENSUS MEETS THE ROW: "command the children of Israel" stands five times in the Torah (Leviticus 24:2; Numbers 5:2, 28:2, 34:2, 35:2),
exactly the row's five. The English "Ibid. 34" hits are EXODUS 34:30 (1:8 — Aaron and Israel afraid of Moses' shining face; the Hebrew row 1:7 carries it
as "ibid. 34" after Exodus 24:17) and DEUTERONOMY 34:1-3 (135:1 on Deuteronomy 3:26 — the LORD showing Moses "all that is called the land of Israel"); the
Hebrew's third "ibid. 34" (118:1, unparenthesized) is Exodus 34:20's firstborn donkey — each read to its verse; the abbreviation's book is the row's
last-named one. The credited row was READ WHOLE at sitting 2 (the Naso ledger num_05_camp_pure_theft — "four readings of 'command'", the exception 34:2
named there) and is CREDITED with a QUICK LOOK. Onkelos 34 whole: 29. No prior ledger had read a row of the chapter; two ledgers NAME a verse of it (the
sotah ledger 34:11 — the border's "reach" verb beside the sotah's "blot"; the vows' exam 34:18): names, not reads — FRESH.

THE READING (ten modules — bor_dump.py the first measurement pass; bor_measure1.py and bor_measure2.py the second and third, printing every candidate
fact (the third for what the second lacked: the Hebrew "ibid." hits read to their books, the pointed forms of the chapter's consonantal homographs, the
full seat lists, the store's gloss families censused); bor_ink.py the ink with every fact an assert (assert_driver.py: EIGHTEEN failures on the first typed
pass, ONE on the second, NONE on the third — bor_legs.py printing every failing leg, each retyped from the print: SIX slice indices (Onkelos 34:4's
"Rekem Geah", Exodus 23:31, Joshua 21:1, 21:6, Numbers 17:21, Onkelos 17:21 — the sixth instance of the lesson), a Joshua-filtered list typed as the
whole-Bible census (the shoulder's twenty-eight seats), a token list's count typed for a verse set's (Samuel's 125 tokens are 120 verses), two hand
tallies against the machine's columns (Joshua's fourteen goings-out typed fifteen; Genesis 46's Manasseh-first missed in the rosters' comparison), the
by-gloss census reaching "according" through the letters of "cord" (the six rewritten glosses' tokens asserted, never a substring family), a prefix
tuple missing the article's forms, and EIGHT POINTED COMPARISONS that fell on THE MARKS' ORDER — the DB's raw order of the vowel points differs from the

logic/oral_triage/num_34_borders_2026-09-12.md (29 sources: Onkelos MATERIAL 29 / CONTEXT 0 — every verse a datum, the two frames the speech-spans';
one Sifrei row credited; coverage computed, missing 0, extra 0; NO cut miss on the writer's first run; gloss_lint 0 on the first write); the display

THE INK, COMPUTED (the measurement passes FIRST, the asserts typed from the print):
- THE PARSER MEASURED FIRST (the standing rule): THREE number verses in twenty-nine, every one read — 34:13 "to the nine tribes and the half tribe" [9]
  (the construct numeral); 34:15 "the two tribes and the half tribe" [2] (the construct "two of" by its points, the caret); 34:18 "one prince, one
  prince from a tribe" [1, 1] — THE DISTRIBUTIVE DOUBLING read as two ones (the same at 13:2's spies and 7:11's dedication, [1, 1, 12] at 17:21's rods;
  Joshua 3:12's stones [12, 1, 1], 22:14's embassy [10, 1, 1]); "half" no numeral before a tribe (34:14 silent, 32:33 silent; Joshua 14:3-4 [2] each);
  no starred homograph; 35:1 empty. NO GAP — the walk's third reading with none. No line of the tape names the chapter.
- THE FRAMES AND THE REGISTER: TWO divine frames — 34:1 (the borders, 34:2-12) and 34:16 (the dividers, 34:17-29), the same five words at both — with
  MOSES' OWN COMMAND between them (34:13-15; the form's two Torah seats 34:13 and 36:5, both in the plains of Moab); THREE narrative verbs; the chapters
  without a frame unchanged (22-24, 29, 30, 32, 36), twelve with exactly two. The border runs on TWENTY "and-it-shall" verbs (34:2-12: "be" 4 + "they
  shall be" 4, "go down" 3, "turn" 2, "pass" 2, "go out" 2, "say", "mark out for yourselves", "reach") and four second-person imperfects; "to you"
  twelve times, and JOSHUA 15:4 KEEPS ONE "TO YOU" inside Judah's border — the spec's pronoun in the run.
- 34:2 THE HEADING: "command the children of Israel" FIVE Torah seats = the Sifrei's five; "when YOU ARE COMING" the participle one seat; "THE LAND
  CANAAN" (the article on the land, none on Canaan) one seat; "THIS IS THE LAND" three Torah seats (34:2, 34:13, Deuteronomy 34:4 from Nebo); THE LOT'S
  VERB "shall fall" with the inheritance at this one Torah seat (Joshua 13:6; Judges 18:1; Ezekiel 47:14 carries the clause; Psalm 16:6) — ONKELOS "shall
  be DIVIDED", one Aramaic verb for 34:2's "fall" and 26:53, 55, 56's "divide"; "BY ITS BORDERS" four Bible seats — the chapter's inclusio (34:2, 12) and
  Joshua's two closers (18:20, 19:49); the border-word SIXTEEN times here, half of Numbers' thirty-two, sixty in the Torah; "as an inheritance" three
  Torah seats, all the land's.
- 34:3-5 THE SOUTH SIDE AND JOSHUA 15:1-4: twenty of the three verses' thirty-three tokens stand in Judah's south border; JOSHUA SPLITS HAZAR-ADDAR (one
  seat) into "Hezron" and "Addar"; Azmon defective here, plene in Joshua. THE SIDE-WORD IS THE TABERNACLE'S (eighteen Torah seats, twelve the court's
  and the boards' sides — Exodus 27:9 "the south side" of the court; Ezekiel gives every side that word, Numbers the south alone). THE SPIES WALKED THE
  BORDER'S LENGTH: "from the wilderness of Zin" with that prefix and the defective "Lebo-hamath" each at exactly two seats — 13:21 and this chapter
  (34:3, 34:8). "On the hands of Edom" one seat, Onkelos "on the borders of"; the Salt Sea nine seats (Genesis 14:3 "that is the Salt Sea" the identity
  idiom); "eastward, toward the sunrise" (34:15) the court's and the camp's phrase; "and it shall turn" the chapter's two and Joshua's five; Kadesh-barnea
  ten seats — ONKELOS "Rekem Geah" at both Numbers seats, apart from Kadesh's "Rekem"; "ITS GOINGS-OUT" — the border's outlet-word: THE TORAH'S FIVE
  SEATS ALL IN THIS CHAPTER, fourteen in Joshua's borders; THE ONE WRITTEN SINGULAR READ PLURAL — 34:4's "and it shall be" the chapter's one
  written-and-read pair (the store's two tokens, the DB's written one), the clause's four other seats written plural, Joshua 15:4 the same clause with
  the written singular; "THE BROOK OF EGYPT" — the bare brook five seats (1 Kings 8:65 "from Lebo-hamath to the brook of Egypt", the border's two ends
  as the kingdom's measure), the covenant's "RIVER of Egypt" another word, and no river, Euphrates, Lebanon or "western sea" of the four promised
  extents stands in the chapter; THE BROOK'S CONSONANTS ARE THE INHERITANCE'S (34:5 against 34:2 — the morphology, the points, Onkelos's two words).
- 34:6-7 THE WEST: the sea-token SEVEN times — the west twice, the great sea twice, the Salt Sea twice, Chinnereth once; ONKELOS SPLITS IT ("the west"
  against "the sea"); "and the west border" two seats (34:6, Joshua 15:12 with the same tail); THE GREAT SEA PLENE at 34:6 AND DEFECTIVE at 34:7 (the
  defective phrase's one seat; the covenant's "great river" defective too); Onkelos adds the possessive to 34:6's second "border".
- 34:7-10 THE NORTH: "YOU SHALL MARK OUT" — THE VERB'S THREE BIBLE SEATS ALL HERE (34:7, 8 Piel; 34:10 Hitpael); its consonants Proverbs' "do not
  DESIRE" (23:3, 6; 24:1), told apart by the points and the morphology; Joshua marks with another verb; ONKELOS "direct yourselves". "The north border"
  two seats, both here. MOUNT HOR twelve Torah seats — ten Aaron's, TWO the north border's (34:7, 8): THE SECOND MOUNT HOR (the chukat runner's data
  row); ONKELOS SPELLS HOR TWO WAYS IN ADJACENT VERSES (without the vav 34:7 and chapters 20-21, with it 34:8 and chapter 33). Zedad two seats (Ezekiel
  47:15), Ziphron one, Hazar-enan four in two spellings; Shepham two, its consonants the leper's "upper lip" (Leviticus 13:45) — the shin's dot against
  the sin's.
- 34:11-12 THE EAST AND THE LOOP: "the Riblah" with the article one seat (the exile's Riblah ten without it); Ain a name at two Torah seats; "AND IT
  SHALL REACH" IS THE BLOTTING VERB — the token's four Bible seats: the sotah's scroll blotted into the water (5:23), a name blotted out (Deuteronomy
  29:19), tears wiped away (Isaiah 25:8), and this border — one pointing at all four; ONKELOS "reach" here, "blot" at 5:23. "THE SHOULDER" — fifteen
  Torah tokens: the court's gate-sides (four), the ephod's shoulder-pieces (nine), the Kohathites' carrying (7:9), and this shore; Joshua's borders say
  it eight times. The sea of Chinnereth two seats, the name seven; ONKELOS "the sea of Gennesar", its one seat. "To the Jordan" with the article and the
  ending the Torah's one seat. THE LOOP: the south opened "from the end of the Salt Sea eastward" (34:3) and the east closes "its goings-out shall be
  the Salt Sea" (34:12); "by its borders round about" the inclusio with 34:2; ONKELOS "round, round" (fifteen seats). EZEKIEL'S BORDERS (47:13-20; 48:1,
  28) share twenty-three tokens, open with 34:13's "you shall inherit" and 34:2's "shall fall to you as an inheritance", and run the sides NORTH, east,
  south, west where Numbers runs south, west, north, east — OBSERVED.
- 34:13-15 MOSES' RESTATEMENT: ONE TRIBE-NOUN — the staff-word "tribe" EIGHTEEN times in the chapter, the other tribe-word NEVER (32:33 gave "the half
  tribe of Manasseh" with the other word; the book's six seats of it; the staff-word 103 times in Numbers); JOSHUA 14:2 QUOTES 34:13's six words "to the
  nine tribes and the half tribe" whole under "as the LORD commanded by the hand of Moses" — the run citing the spec outside the Torah — and 13:7 says
  them with the other word; "NINE" in the construct three Bible seats, every one the nine tribes; "you shall inherit" the reflexive stem's Torah seats
  (Leviticus 25:46; 32:18; 33:54 twice; 34:13), Ezekiel 47:13 the same; "by lot" four Torah seats, fifteen in the Bible; "THE REUBENITE ... THE GADITE"
  — THE PAIR'S FIRST SEAT, thirteen in the Bible; "the half tribe of Manasseh" with the staff-word here and in the Levite cities' rows (Joshua 21:5, 6,
  27; 1 Chronicles 6:56); "THEY TOOK THEIR INHERITANCE" four Bible seats — 34:14, 34:15, Joshua 13:8, 18:7 — always the two and a half; "beyond the
  Jordan at Jericho" two seats (22:1, 34:15). ONKELOS: one Aramaic tribe-word for both Hebrew words (eighteen here; 32:33's "half tribe" rendered as
  34:14's), "tribes" in the plural at 34:13 and 34:15 alone.
- 34:16-18 THE COMMISSION: "THESE ARE THE NAMES OF THE MEN" four Bible seats, all Numbers' rosters (1:5, 13:16, 34:17, 34:19); THE ONE ROOT IN THREE
  STEMS — "inherit" eight times in the chapter: the reflexive (34:13), the plain (34:17, 18), the INTENSIVE (34:29), the nouns (34:2, 14, 15 — and
  34:5's brook); THE INTENSIVE STEM'S FOUR BIBLE SEATS — 34:29 and three RUNS in Joshua (13:32 Moses'; 14:1 and 19:51 "which Eleazar the priest and
  Joshua son of Nun ... divided"); the plain stem's "to divide the land" runs at Joshua 19:49 ("they finished dividing the land by its borders"); THE
  OBJECT SWITCHES WITH THE STEM — the land at 34:17-18, the people at 34:29. "ELEAZAR THE PRIEST AND JOSHUA SON OF NUN" as one phrase three seats —
  34:17 and Joshua's two runs (14:1, 19:51). "ONE PRINCE, ONE PRINCE from a tribe" — THE DISTRIBUTIVE DOUBLING of the spies (13:2), the rods (17:21), the
  dedication (7:11), Joshua's stones (3:12, 4:2, 4:4) and Phinehas's embassy (22:14). ONKELOS: "one chief, one chief" as at 7:11 and 17:21 — and one
  Aramaic word, "great", for the great sea (34:6, 7) and the prince (34:18, 22-28); one form for the plain and the intensive stems; the possession-root
  at all seven inheritance tokens.

  "without lying-in-wait", "any stone whereby he may die", "without seeing", "and he dropped it on him", "not his enemy", "seeking his harm" one seat
  each; "the congregation shall judge between the smiter and the avenger of blood" one seat, "these judgments" here and Deuteronomy 7:12; THE
  CONGREGATION-TOKEN four times (12, 24, 25, 25) — the Sifrei's "three in the section" the judgment's; "the congregation shall deliver", "from the hand
  of the avenger", "return him", "to his city of refuge where he fled" one seat each; "UNTIL THE DEATH OF THE HIGH PRIEST" 35:25, 28 and Joshua 20:6
  PLENE — THE HIGH PRIEST WRITTEN DEFECTIVE ONLY HERE (35:25, 28 twice; plene at sixteen seats, "and the high priest" at Leviticus 21:10 and 2 Kings
  12:11); "WHO WAS ANOINTED WITH THE HOLY OIL" one seat, "the anointing oil" the tabernacle's nine, ONKELOS' anointing-verb one seat in the book; "IF THE
  SLAYER GOING OUT GOES OUT" — the doubled infinitive's two Bible seats: Jacob's from Isaac (Genesis 27:30) and this; SIX doubled infinitives in the
  chapter; "the border of his city of refuge", "outside the border", "the avenger finds him" one each; "HE HAS NO BLOOD" — THE BURGLAR'S ACQUITTAL
  (Exodus 22:1, the plural; 22:2 "he has blood") at its second seat, the singular; "the land of his possession" one seat (the family six); JOSHUA 20:6
  ADDS "and come to his city and to his house".
- 35:29-34 THE STATUTE, THE WITNESSES, THE RANSOM, THE LAND: "A STATUTE OF JUDGMENT" — the phrase's two Bible seats: THE DAUGHTERS' (27:11) and this

  (Exodus 21:30), THE HALF-SHEKEL'S (30:12), this chapter's two; "wicked to die" one seat; "for he shall surely die" one; "until the death of the priest"

THE DESIGN'S DECISIONS (the box's (a)-(m) settled on the measurements):
(a) THE BARE DUAL THOUSAND — rule (29) of the parser, BY THE POINTS ON THE STEM (1b's law at a fifth stem): verse_words emits the dual אַלְפַּיִם ("two
thousand" — a sheva under the lamed) with the dual mark (the tilde the dual nouns carry since 3b: אלפים~ "two thousand, marked"), and ink_numbers reads the marked dual as 2,000
WHEREVER IT STANDS — bare before a unit noun, a noun of persons, or with the approximation prefix; the UNMARKED plural אֲלָפִים ("thousands" — a qamats
under the lamed) with no unit numeral before it is a NOUN and reads nothing (the old rule read it as 2,000 when a numeral followed — no true seat had that
shape, and Psalm 8:8's "oxen" fell through it). NINE probes to FAIL first (35:5 = [2000, 2000, 2000, 2000]; Joshua 3:4 = [2000] — the cubit consumed;
Joshua 7:3 = [2000, 3000]; Judges 20:45 = [5000, 2000]; 1 Samuel 13:2 = [3000, 2000, 1000]; 1 Kings 7:26 = [2000]; 2 Kings 18:23 = [2000]; Isaiah 36:8 =
[2000]; Psalm 8:8 = [] — the false reading) and NINE regressions unmoved (Num 4:36 [2750], Exod 38:29 [70, 2400], Dan 8:14 [2300], Num 7:85 [130, 1, 70,
1, 2400], Exod 18:21 [100, 50, 10], Num 1:46 [603550], Exod 32:28 [3000], Num 26:62 [23000], Ezra 2:3 [2172]); THE CORPUS DIFF old against new over every

cubits" PAID by the teaching. Two kin readings stay filed (Ezekiel 45:2's join across "by"; Exodus 27:9's linen — a homograph by context); one more filed
